\section{Proof spine}
All routes from an arbitrary polynomial-time decider to a superpolynomial lower bound remain \OPEN{}.

The current strongest boundary synthesis is now exact on both sides of the
easy-root objection.  ROOT-HARD-PROJECTION129 gives an NP-hard unpinned root
whose exponentially many pinned labels are perfectly correlated.
BOUNDARY-ORACLE-FACTOR128 generalizes its threshold to every explicit
nonempty support circuit \(C\):
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(H(F,C)[b])=C(b)\wedge\operatorname{SAT}(F),\qquad
 \operatorname{SAT}(H(F,C))=\operatorname{SAT}(F).
\]
Every deterministic adaptive boundary-query transcript is simulated by
evaluating \(C\) and reading/caching the one source bit
\(\operatorname{SAT}(F)\) at most once.  Thus support cardinality, density,
Hamming connectivity, component count, ANF degree and the number of
source-complete pinned queries do not supply independent-work amplification.
The supported ordinary child retains source-scale length, so
\(\lambda=1\).  Role~3's lazy source-backed handle avoids materialization but
does not shorten that ordinary encoding.  Role~4's scoped padding raises a
conditional length floor and cannot shrink it; its concurrent
SCOPE-CLIQUE-FIREWALL88 proves that padding/reordering cannot create
sublogarithmic source-preserving width on a matched clique family, while a
cheap recognizer shows that large scoped width is not machine space.
Role~1's interval-factor algorithm and Role~3's typed degree-two
extension are restricted polynomial upper bounds; Role~3's connected
degree-three image is NP-complete but supplies no mandatory-work theorem.
Role~2's exact union scheduler removes duplicate named-boundary pairings.
Role~3's expander-stream theorem forces a large source frontier in every
clause order on its family, while its paid affine recognizer decides the same
instances and blocks a universal frontier-cost inference.  Role~5's
NAMED-FRONTIER-COMPOSITION132 independently composes these ingredients on the
STRICT-SUBJOIN matched pair: exact source-name relations recover both labels
at two recursion levels, whereas block SAT bits do not.  Union scheduling
reduces finite generated-child volume by more than a factor ten, but the
largest ordinary child remains larger than the source.  The highest-value
missing theorem is still an all-input, same-realization service with a
jointly favorable achievable pair, genuinely
\(N^{\lambda+o(1)}\) ordinary children for \(\lambda<1\) when that route
is used, and exact paid parent gluing.

The fixed-width implication chain gives the exact base rung: its formula is
UNSAT iff every orientation field is one, and every deterministic exact
field-query decider reads all $q$ orientations on the all-one input.  Under
the declared fixed-width encoding this is an $\Omega(N/\log N)$ word-probe
acquisition bound, not an unrestricted time theorem.

For
\[
F(X,Y)=T(X)\wedge B(X,Y),
\]
where $B$ is 2-CNF, $T$ has at most $k$ clauses of width at most three, and
$X$ is the set of variables occurring in $T$, let $s=|X|\le3k$.
The exact 2-CNF projection $Q_B(X)\equiv\exists Y\,B$ has at most $2s^2$
unit/binary clauses.  One SCC computation plus reachability traversals from
the $2s$ signed boundary literals constructs it in
\[
O((1+s)(n+m)+s^2)=O(k(n+m)+k^2)
\]
word time.  The exact kernel $T\wedge Q_B$ has at most $3k$ variables and
$k+18k^2$ clauses.  This is a restricted upper bound and transport barrier,
not a lower bound.

DENSE-COUPLING-FIREWALL90 gives equal-size 3-coloring labels with source
treewidth exactly $t$ and one-hot-CNF primal treewidth in $[t,3t+2]$, yet a
canonical $O(N/\log N)$ source scan decides the label.  Density, unbounded
treewidth, large syntactic residual, essential constraints, and padding do
not alone force expensive continuation.

AGGREGATION-CEILING91 now closes the independent-composition rung.  For $p$
chain blocks with $q$ orientation fields each and any read-once AND/OR
aggregation tree, a linear-size guarded width-three CNF has the exact aggregate
SAT label, while its deterministic orientation-field complexity is exactly
$pq$.  The proof uses full multilinear degree $pq$.  A constant-overhead map
reaches ordinary proper 3CNF.  However the source already contains $pq$
independent fields and the target length is $\Theta((pq+p)\log N)$, so the
bound is only $\Theta(N/\log N)$ acquisition.  Independent products, disjoint
components, and guarded selectors therefore do not amplify beyond input order.

SHARED-INTERFACE-TABLE92 tests the first explicit shared-interface rung.
For a $J\times 2^h$ feasibility table, the parent label is
\[
\bigvee_{a\in\{0,1\}^h}\bigwedge_{j=1}^J r_{j,a}.
\]
Its exact deterministic orientation-field depth is $J2^h$: the unique
multilinear polynomial contains the full monomial.  A fixed-metadata map to
ordinary proper 3CNF has $\Theta(J2^h h)$ clauses and variables.  This still
does not amplify: the source already contains $J2^h$ fields, paid construction
cost is $\Theta(J2^h h)$, and direct inclusion preserves only input-order
acquisition.  Succinct shared compatibility and all-decider continuation
remain OPEN.

RELATION-ALGEBRA-BOUNDARY93 separates the next succinct relation rung.
Three-valued permutation constraints $x_v=x_u+b\pmod3$ admit exact linear
holonomy normalization on arbitrary graphs, regardless of cycle count or
treewidth.  For arbitrary explicit binary relations, a supplied feedback
vertex set of size $c$ gives an exact $O(3^c(|V|+|E|))$ continuation
algorithm; a cycle plus $h$ chords has $c\le h+1$.  Replacing functional
relations by inequality reaches graph 3-coloring, but this relation-language
inclusion transports no superlinear bound.  The affine source bound is
consumed by linear construction or preserved only at input-acquisition order.

EXCEPTION-KERNEL94 gives the charged interpolation between those endpoints.
After contracting all permutation records, $p$ arbitrary binary-relation
exceptions leave at most $2p$ active three-valued roots.  Taking one endpoint
per exception gives a vertex cover of size $c\le p$, hence an exact
$O(N+3^c p)$-word algorithm with linear peak space.  The one-hot decision
kernel has at most $6p$ Boolean variables and $23p$ clauses of width at most
three.  A matched family forces exactly $p$ relation-field reads, but a direct
$O(p)$ scan matches it, so the bound remains input-order acquisition.

SELECTOR-GRAPH-BOUNDARY95 now makes the domain/structure progression exact.
Arbitrary binary CSP over domain two compiles with at most four binary clauses
per relation and is therefore 2-SAT.  For domain $d$, a forest constraint
graph is solved in $O(ed^2+gd)$ word time, and a supplied feedback vertex set
of size $c$ gives $O(ed^2+gd+d^c(ed^2+gd))$ time with linear peak space.
At domain three, restricting relations to inequality gives graph 3-coloring.
The compatible CNF has $2dg$ variables, $g(d-1)$ genuine ternaries, and at
most $g(\binom d2+2d)+ed^2$ binary clauses.  A two-selector table can force
$d^2$ field reads, but a table scan matches that input-order bound.

PALETTE-SOURCE-CYCLE56 and DEGREE-BOUNDARY96 now sharpen direct inclusion.
The first gives a proper exactly-three-clause family whose parity orientation
records force \(q=\Omega(N/\log N)\) word reads on both labels, with a matching
linear recognizer.  The second locates the exact graph rung: by Brooks,
maximum-degree-three 3-colorability is decided in linear time by detecting
\(K_4\) components, while maximum-degree-four 3-colorability is known
NP-complete.  A \(p\)-block equal-metadata family inside the latter fixed
language forces every exact decider to read \(p=\Theta(N/\log N)\) packed edge
words on its SAT input.  This identity transport avoids construction cost,
but the degree-three scan still matches it in \(O(p)\) time.  Hence direct
inclusion preserves a genuine all-decider acquisition bound and still does
not amplify beyond input order.

HORN-SELECTOR-DEGREE60 and HORN-TRANSPORT-CEILING100 now add the
sharp exact-three occurrence rung.  A source degree bound of three makes
every instance satisfiable by a clause-saturating Hall matching, while the
degree-four canonical Horn-selector language is NP-complete.  For the padded
implication-chain image, flipping orientation $i$ changes exactly two
disjoint target consequent words, so every exact target-query decider reads
$q=\Theta(N/\log N)$ fields on the all-one target.  However explicit
materialization writes $32q+130$ words, giving transport numerator at most
$q-(32q+130)-H<0$.  Direct sensitivity survives; paid transport and any
amplification beyond input order do not.  The chain image is in the
unrestricted canonical language, not the maximum-degree-four sublanguage.

ADAPTIVE-TRAJECTORY-FIREWALL107 adds the required state-dependent correction.
There is a coupled both-label family \(H_{q,b}\) with
\[
 k_0=4(q+1),\qquad h_0=q+1,
\]
where \(h_0\) is the minimum raw strong syntactic 2-CNF backdoor.  A total
uniform exact policy pays to discover source-certified binary resolvents,
propagates them, and reaches \(k_1=h_1=0\) in \(O(q)\) word time.  UNSAT
terminates on an observed conflict; SAT switches to SCC and replays one
witness.  Unsupported states select Horn or exact fallback, with failed
tests and switches charged.  Thus neither initial \(k\), initial \(h\), nor
one fixed rule models every adaptive solver.  The missing invariant must
cover the full paid state trajectory, arbitrary representation changes,
SAT/UNSAT asymmetry and retained cache.

For every proposed reduction, only
\[
T_{\rm new}^{\le}(g(q))\ge
\frac{L_{\rm old}(q)-R(q)-H(q)}{Q(q)\tau(q)}
\]
is justified, and only with a positive numerator.  Linear construction or
recognition consumes the linear chain/direct-sum bound.  Direct inclusion
preserves the same query order but supplies no amplification.

\section{BACKDOOR-PRODUCT-FIREWALL97 update}
Role~4's shared-switch family can be replicated in $p$ variable-disjoint
components.  The resulting proper 3-CNF has
\[
n=p(q+4),\qquad m=p(2q+4),
\]
and its minimum strong syntactic 2-CNF backdoor is exactly $h=p$: each
component requires one hit and its private switch suffices.  Nevertheless
the product is SAT exactly when all $p$ component parities are even, so a
charged recognizer and source scan decides it in $O(pq)$ word operations.
With fixed $q=3$, $h=n/7=\Theta(N/\log N)$ while decision remains
input-linear.  Thus even a linear minimum backdoor, essential ternaries and
disjoint-product amplification do not force $2^{\Omega(h)}$ work.

On the all-even product, pointwise differential coverage forces every exact
deterministic decider to inspect at least one field from each of the $pq$
disjoint orientation pairs.  This is only
$\Omega(N/\log N)$ word acquisition and is matched by the scan.  A paid
materializing reduction has $R\ge p(2q+4)>L_{\rm old}=pq$, so its transport
remainder is nonpositive before target simulation.  Direct inclusion
preserves but cannot amplify the input-order bound.  The independent audit
recorded 285 assertion groups, 127,488 exhaustive assignments and zero
failures.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-23_1948_MAIN_BACKDOOR_PRODUCT_FIREWALL97.tex}.

\section{CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98 update}
Let \(G\) be any finite simple 2-vertex-connected cubic graph with \(g\)
vertices and \(e=3g/2\) edges.  Put one Boolean source variable on each edge
and encode the parity equation at each vertex by its four proper ternary
exclusion clauses.  The resulting \(\mathcal T(G,b)\) has
\[
n=e=3g/2,\qquad k=m=4g,\qquad
N=(16g+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\right\rceil .
\]
It is SAT iff the total charge is even, with \(2^{g/2+1}\) models.
Every odd-charge member is clause-minimal UNSAT; deleting any one clause
leaves exactly \(2^{g/2-1}\) models.  Strong syntactic-2CNF backdoors are
exactly edge covers.  Petersen's theorem supplies a perfect matching, so
the minimum backdoor is \(h=g/2=n/3=k/8\).

Changing one vertex charge reverses the label and changes only its disjoint
four-clause sign group.  Thus every deterministic exact decider performs at
least \(g=\Omega(N/\log N)\) addressed source-word reads on every family
member.  A canonical recognizer and parity scan matches this in \(O(g)\)
word operations.  These are \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped} facts.

For cubic graph families with \(\operatorname{tw}(G)=\Omega(g)\), the
It\-syk\-son--Riazanov--Smirnov theorem gives odd-charge Tseitin formulas
regular-resolution size \(2^{\Omega(g)}\), while the same formulas still
have the linear charge decider.  Therefore transport from exponential
regular-resolution proof length to arbitrary deterministic SAT-decision
time is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} absent a new all-decider simulation
theorem.  The restricted proof-system lower bound remains valid.
The stdlib audit covered six graphs with 84,366 assertion-accounted checks,
126,144 assignment checks, 168 single-clause deletions and zero failures.
See
\path{research/main/2026-10-23_2048_MAIN_CUBIC_AFFINE_FIREWALL98.tex}.

\section{KROM-PROJECTION-CEILING99 update}
For any 2-CNF \(F(X,Y)\) with \(s=|X|\ge1\), the exact existential
projection \(\exists Y\,F\) has an auxiliary-free 2-CNF with at most
\(2s^2\) distinct non-tautological unit/binary clauses.  Davis--Putnam
elimination preserves width two, and only \(2s\) units plus
\(2s(s-1)\) non-tautological binaries exist.  Thus recursive 2-CNF
mediators cannot force superquadratic final CNF size in the retained
boundary.

Role~4's source
\[
M_p=\bigwedge_i(\neg a_i\vee u)\land
    \bigwedge_j(\neg u\vee\neg b_j)
\]
is asymptotically tight: projection gives all \(p^2\) clauses
\((\neg a_i\vee\neg b_j)\), and every equivalent auxiliary-free CNF,
even with arbitrary widths, needs at least \(p^2\) clauses.  Yet the same
function has the two-term DNF
\[
(\bigwedge_i\neg a_i)\vee(\bigwedge_j\neg b_j),
\]
the original linear CNF with one retained mediator, and an \(O(p)\) scan.
The \(\Omega(p^2)\) output is therefore \textbf{PROVED/EXACT} but
representation-relative.  Recursive Krom forgetting and transport from
mandatory auxiliary-free CNF output to arbitrary one-bit decision are
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.

The independent audit recorded 3,526 assertion-accounted checks, all
clauses through \(p=4\) with exact minima \(1,4,9,16\), 320 random Krom
projections, 2,840 projected assignments, Role~4 weighted model counts
through \(p=8\), 1,048,576 full matched-source assignments, and zero
failures.  The Role~4 count
\(64\cdot7^{p-1}3^p\) on SAT and zero on UNSAT is confirmed.
Role~3's exponential nonlinear projection likewise retains compact
DNF/existential-CNF/scan bypasses.  Role~4's affine-matching plus positive
ternary language remains a parsimonious NP-complete endpoint, not a time
lower bound.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-23_2148_MAIN_KROM_PROJECTION_CEILING99.tex}.

\section{HORN-TRANSPORT-CEILING100 update}
For proper exact-three source $\Phi$ with $m$ clauses, the selector lift
\[
H(\Phi)=\bigwedge_j[(s_{j1}\vee s_{j2}\vee s_{j3})
\wedge\bigwedge_{a=1}^{3}(\neg s_{ja}\vee\ell_{ja})]
\]
is equisatisfiable, has $n=n_0+3m$ variables and $4m$ clauses, and has
minimum raw strong syntactic 2-CNF and Horn backdoors $m$ and $2m$.
If every source variable occurs at most three times, Hall matching constructs
a witness; at degree four the canonical language is a
\textbf{KNOWN NP-complete} endpoint.  Linear backdoors therefore remain a
hostile control rather than a lower bound.

Padding the $q$-orientation implication chain and applying this lift gives
$n=8q+31$, $M=8q+32$, and $W=32q+130$ explicit words.  The all-one target
is UNSAT; flipping orientation $i$ makes it SAT and changes exactly two
signed consequent words.  The $q$ pairs are disjoint, so the target family
has a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT, encoding-scoped} $q=\Theta(N/\log N)$
word/record query lower bound.  A source-aware linear recognizer matches it.
Paid one-call transport is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} because
\[
L_{\rm old}-R-H\le q-(32q+130)-H<0.
\]
The independent audit checked all 254 chain orientations through $q=7$,
600 degree-three Hall instances, 1,981 clauses, exact backdoors through
$m=3$, and 2,624 assertion-accounted conditions with zero failures:
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.  The lifted chain's endpoint variables have source
occurrence degree six, so it is not silently placed in the degree-four
sublanguage.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-23_2248_MAIN_HORN_TRANSPORT_CEILING100.tex},
\path{experiments/review/horn_transport_ceiling100_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/review/HORN_TRANSPORT_CEILING100_raw.json}.


\section{PORTFOLIO-PRISM-FIREWALL101 update}
On the fixed NP-complete language of proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF,
CUBICCOVER75 and BLOSSOM-FACTOR60 are total exact algorithms with independent
source parameters $\delta$ and $t$.  Alternating one step of each and
returning the first answer gives the exact charged portfolio bound
\[
O\!\left(\min\left\{
m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+2^{\lfloor5\delta/8\rfloor}(n+m+1),\,
N/w+m\log(m+2)+3^t(n+m)^3
\right\}\right).
\]
Hence $\delta=O(\log N)$ or $t=O(\log N)$ is a polynomial region.  This is a
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT upper bound}, not necessary work.

The connected prism family $G_q$ has $n=6q$, $m=8q$, exact signed occurrence
$(2,2)$, complementary-pair defect $\delta=2q$, and paired-support defect
$t=8q$.  Orienting both prism rings cyclically and setting all auxiliary
cycle variables false gives a linear-time witness.  A canonical total exact
decider recognizes this family in $O(N/w)$ word time and falls back to
exhaustive SAT otherwise.  Therefore linear $\delta$, linear $t$, and their
minimum as a per-instance exponential lower bound are
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.  Role~3's all-order exponential HWB OBDD bound
has the same all-decider ceiling: a polynomial template/HWB decider need not
build the OBDD.

The independent audit generated 62 connected sources for $3\le q\le64$,
checked both-label controls and 684 assertion-accounted conditions, and had
zero failures: \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-23_2348_MAIN_PORTFOLIO_PRISM_FIREWALL101.tex},
\path{experiments/review/portfolio_prism_firewall101_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/review/PORTFOLIO_PRISM_FIREWALL101_raw.json}.

\section{SUPPORT-SIGN-FIREWALL102 update}
Every simple 4-regular 3-uniform clause support has a polynomially
constructible exact signed-$(2,2)$ signing satisfied by all TRUE.  Give each
variable two clones.  For every clause set $Q$, degree four gives
$2|N(Q)|\ge 3|Q|/2\ge |Q|$, so Hall's theorem matches every clause to one
clone.  Make matched occurrences positive and fill each variable to two
positives.  This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT}.

Applied to DOECKER-CORE-CERT61's verified 198-variable, 264-clause
support-simple UNSAT source, the construction gives a SAT formula with the
same ordered unsigned supports, metadata, record count, and encoded length;
all TRUE is a witness and 398 sign fields differ.  Hence every invariant of
the complete unsigned incidence representation---including deficiency,
width, expansion, separators, and clause-dual geometry---is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} as a stand-alone label obstruction.

For $p$ disjoint blocks independently equal to the SAT or UNSAT template, the
conjunction is SAT exactly when all blocks are SAT.  On the all-SAT input,
every exact deterministic sign-field, literal-word, or clause-record decision
tree in the fixed block-major encoding needs $p$ queries.  Here
$n=198p$, $m=264p$, $W=1056p+2$, and
$p=\Theta(N/\log N)$.  This is a \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted query
lower bound}, not a machine-time theorem: a template scan is linear.  A paid
reduction from the $p$-field chain writes at least $1056p+2$ target words, so
$L_{\rm old}-R-H<0$ and transport has no positive remainder.

REGULAR-DEFICIENCY63 was independently checked: the full clause set is the
unique maximum-deficiency source set, one-clause deletion lowers the maximum
exactly by one, and incomplete Horn-selector groups cannot create equality.
These are structural facts only.  The audit recorded 210 checks and zero
failures.  See

\section{FROZEN-SIGN-FLOW103 update}
CAPACITY-SIGNING70's proposed partial frozen-sign experiment has a complete
candidate solution, pending independent peer red-team.  Fix an assignment,
per-variable sign quotas, and arbitrary frozen occurrence signs.  If $f_v$ is
the frozen-true count, $c_v$ the required true-occurrence quota, and $h_C$ the
frozen-true count in clause $C$, the unfrozen true incidences satisfy
\[
 \sum_{e\ni v}x_e=c_v-f_v,\qquad
 \sum_{e\in C}x_e\ge \max\{0,1-h_C\},\qquad x_e\in\{0,1\}.
\]
This is a lower-bounded bipartite flow of linear network size.  Minimum
sign-field Hamming repair for the fixed assignment is integral min-cost
circulation.  Status is \textbf{CANDIDATE}: complete proofs and executable
checks are present, but independent review is pending.

A second candidate theorem gives a closed form.  Every proper monotone
exact-$(2,2)$ 3-CNF with $m$ clauses has minimum distance exactly $m$ from the
balanced signings on the same ordered unsigned records that all TRUE
satisfies.  One Hall matching chooses a distinct variable in every negative
clause; a capacity-two Hall matching chooses the compensating positive
occurrences while leaving every positive clause satisfied.  The verified
198-variable, 264-clause D\"ocker UNSAT source therefore has an optimum
all-true repair at distance 264: 132 flips in each direction.  The distance
398 construction in MAIN102 remains correct but is now known nonoptimal.

The audit's min-cost implementation preserved all ordered unsigned supports,
replayed the repaired witness, and agreed with exhaustive reference minima on
all 216 balanced signings under each of eight assignments.  It recorded 44
assertion-accounted checks and zero failures: \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-24_0148_MAIN_FROZEN_SIGN_FLOW103.tex},
\path{experiments/review/frozen_sign_flow103_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/review/FROZEN_SIGN_FLOW103_raw.json}.

This closes only the fixed-witness compatibility phase.  The repair may change
UNSAT to SAT and is not a SAT-preserving reduction.  EXCLUSIVEQUOTIENT78's
20-clause necessary UNSAT obstruction and DUALPAIR61's orthogonal optional
defects remain useful certified upper-bound structure, but arbitrary deciders
need not materialize any of them.  P versus NP remains open.



\subsection*{Concurrent specialist reconciliation: TUTTECOVER79 and EULER-NAE-DOUBLE65}
TUTTECOVER79's sharp loopless cubic-multigraph theorem was independently
checked from its Tutte--Berge proof.  For every such multigraph on $s$
vertices,
\[
 \nu(H)\ge\lceil2s/5\rceil,
\]
and the ten-vertex three-lobe multigraph attains equality.  Applied to the
existing complementary-pair contraction, this gives a source-variable hit
set of size at most $\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor$ and the charged exact upper
bound
\[
 O\!\left(m\log(m+2)+\delta^4+
 2^{\lfloor3\delta/5\rfloor}(n+m+1)\right)
\]
with linear reusable space.  This is now \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}
after Role~5 proof review; the reported 553-check finite audit remains
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.  It improves the prior general $5\delta/8$ exponent
but is an upper algorithm, not necessary work.

EULER-NAE-DOUBLE65 was also independently proof-checked and its self-contained
mini-auditor rerun (95 cases, zero failures).  Pairing each even-degree
variable's occurrence sockets and balancing the derived clause multigraph by
Euler orientation constructs, in linear word time, a quota-balanced signing
satisfied by any prescribed assignment and its complement.  For a monotone
exact-$(2,2)$ source, one cubic bipartite perfect matching gives the stronger
two-witness repair at the same optimal distance $m$.  These statements are
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the sign-construction model}; they change the signed
input and provide no SAT-decision lower bound.  The earlier MAIN103 candidate
is thereby strengthened for monotone sources, while arbitrary frozen-sign
completion remains only at its previously reviewed status.

\subsection*{SWAP-STAR-CEILING104: exact local-sensitivity ceiling}
Fresh Role~4 TWO-SIGN-SWAP64, Role~3 SWAPQUARTET71 and Role~1
ANTIPODAL79 have now been independently synthesized.  For a valid signed
exact-$(2,2)$ source $F$, let $G_x(F)$ join the two positive and two negative
occurrence positions of $x$ exactly when their legal sign swap reverses the
SAT label.  On the explicit promise containing $F$ and all its
opposite-label one-swap neighbors, the deterministic sign-field query depth
is exactly
\[
 D({\cal S}(F))=\sum_x\tau(G_x(F))
 =\sum_x\nu(G_x(F))\le2n.
\]
The lower bound is the transcript/vertex-cover argument; the upper algorithm
queries a minimum cover, which distinguishes the center from every offered
neighbor.  Thus SWAP-COVER71 is independently verified and promoted
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT} in the addressed sign-field promise model.  The $2n$
bound is a ceiling on this radius-one adversary, not an upper bound on SAT
runtime.

The concrete 198-variable, 264-clause U/V pair was replayed: two sign fields
change, all ordered unsigned supports and exact quotas remain fixed, and the
supplied assignment satisfies all 264 repaired clauses while falsifying
exactly one original clause.  The original UNSAT certificate remains an
imported DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 dependency.  The repaired formula is mixed-sign,
not strictly monotone.  The standard-library audit covered all 16 subgraphs
of $K_{2,2}$, 272 exact one/two-variable star decision trees and 4,096
three-variable graph tuples: 8,778 assertion-accounted checks, zero failures
(\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}).

The four-context extraction is an optional general-proper-3SAT reduction:
its children generally leave the narrow exact-$(2,2)$ grammar, and no
one-bit decider is compelled to materialize those contexts.  ANTIPODAL79 and
MAIN103 solve supplied sign-edit objectives by polynomial flow but change
the source instance.  The disjoint U/V product still has a matching
$O(p)$ template scan.  Hence local swap sensitivity, sign-edit distance and
Johnson-product geometry do not yield a post-acquisition lower bound.

\subsection*{ANTIPODAL-CUT-FIREWALL105: linear dual-certificate ceiling}
Role~4's FROZEN-ANTIPODAL-CUT66 has been independently proved and
computationally replayed.  For a supplied assignment \(a\), frozen occurrence
signs, and exact \((2,2)\) variable quotas, simultaneous satisfaction by
\(a,\bar a\) is exactly an integral lower-bounded circulation.  Writing
\(R_v=2-p_v\), \(L_C=\max(0,1-t_C)\), and
\(U_C=\min(f_C,2-t_C)\), feasibility is equivalent to
\[
 R(X)\le e_f(X,{\cal C}\setminus Y)+U(Y),\qquad
 L(Y)\le R(V\setminus X)+e_f(X,Y)
\]
for every \(X\subseteq V,Y\subseteq{\cal C}\).
Every YES instance has a free-socket certificate of at most \(3m\) bits;
every NO instance has one failed local interval or a violated cut encoded
by \(n+m\) bits.  Both are checked in \(O(n+m)\) word operations, and
max-flow extracts one without subset enumeration.  This is
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT} in the supplied-witness sign-completion model.

The seven-pin support passes every local test but violates the cut
\(2\le0+1\), so local quotas are insufficient.  The independent audit
enumerated all \(46{,}656\) balanced signings, found \(5{,}904\) antipodal
completions, and compared brute feasibility with all \(16{,}384\) cut pairs
on 32 seeded pinned instances; zero failures (\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}).
The operation changes signs and receives the witness, so it is not an
unchanged-input SAT-preserving reduction.  Polynomial primal/dual
certificates are therefore a hostile control, not an all-decider lower
bound.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-24_0348_MAIN_ANTIPODAL_CUT_FIREWALL105.tex},
\path{experiments/review/antipodal_cut_firewall105_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/review/ANTIPODAL_CUT_FIREWALL105_raw.json}.

\subsection*{C5-SOS-FIREWALL106: exact degree-four obstruction is not a SAT-label invariant}
Role~4's DEGREE4-C5 report has been independently audited.  In the one-hot
graph-colouring equality encoding, two exact degree-four identities force
\(\widetilde{\mathbb E}[Y]=2\) on every five-cycle, where \(Y\) counts
same-colour chord pairs.  The predecessor canonical degree-two functional
instead has
\[
 \widetilde{\mathbb E}_2[Y]=\frac53+\frac{J}{12}\ge\frac{25}{12}>2,
\]
so it cannot extend to degree four on any source containing a five-cycle.
This nonextension is a representation failure, not a label signal: the bare
\(C_5\) is SAT, has 30 labelled 3-colourings, and every colouring has \(Y=2\).

The paired 12-vertex controls independently enumerate to zero and 108
colourings and contain 44 and 28 five-cycles.  Six exact orbit equations have
rank six and force pair moments
\[
 (t,6t-2,t,2-4t,2-4t,t,t).
\]
PSD tests require \(t\ge7/18\) and \(t\le1/4\), so the UNSAT control has no
degree-four pseudoexpectation in the stated equality encoding.  The
independent standard-library audit reconstructed both local polynomial
identities and made 494 exact assertions with zero failures.  It did not
replay the separate 2.67MB, 1,776-square static certificate; that larger
artifact is not independently promoted here.

This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}.  It neither supplies a growing
lower bound nor covers arbitrary deciders.  A fixed core is constant, and
disjoint replication permits linear template/component recognition.
GAUGEFORK81's sharp \(2t\) promise query bound and REPAIR-GAP64's unbounded
LP gap provide aligned hostile controls: large source or relaxation geometry
can coexist with near-linear direct decision.  See

orientation produces a binary residual, SCC solution and original-source

This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}.  It falsifies any universal
\(2^{a h}/N^b\) or \(2^{a k}/N^b\) lower bound based only on the initial
scalar.  It does not exclude a trajectory-wide invariant that survives every
sound switch.  A general recognizer-cascade lemma now charges parsing, every
failed recognizer, policy evaluation, switching, chosen continuation,
verification and either discarded or retained live state.  It is an exact
upper-bound closure rule, not finite-menu coverage of all deciders.

The independent audit checked all 62 \(H_{q,b}\) inputs through \(q=5\),

\subsection*{Weighted adaptive transcript ceiling (WEIGHTED-TRANSCRIPT-COVER108)}

ADAPTIVE-COVER75's unit-cost conditional-read potential extends exactly to
heterogeneous source actions.  For a fixed action schema with costs \(c(a)>0\),
let opposite-label sources consistent with the current transcript contribute
their sets of distinguishing actions, and let \(\tau_c\) be the minimum-cost
hitting set.  Every total deterministic exact decider must spend at least
\(\tau_c\) on future new informative source actions.  If its next new action
is \(a\), then
\[
 0\le \tau_c(S_t)-\tau_c(S_{t+1})\le c(a).
\]
Thus the valid trajectory accounting telescopes:
\[
 \tau_c(S_0)\le\sum_{\text{new queried }a}c(a)
 \le\sum_{a\in\mathcal A}c(a).
\]
Summing the residual values themselves is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.  After
the complete declared source is acquired, this potential is zero; it cannot
supply the missing post-acquisition lower bound.

The exact unit-free equality-quotient family sharpens the SAT/UNSAT split.
An equality 2-CNF backbone on \(3q\) source variables plus \(q\) disjoint
all-positive/all-negative ternaries is SAT iff all orientations are equal.
It has no unit clauses and raw strong 2-CNF backdoor \(h=q\).  With orientation
costs \(c_i\), a uniform SAT input has
\(\tau_c=\sum_i c_i\), while a mixed UNSAT input has
\[
 \tau_c=\min_{i:b_i=0}c_i+\min_{j:b_j=1}c_j.
\]
Paid SCC equivalence collapses all ternaries to representative units and
solves the recognized family in linear time and live space.  Promise-only
early stopping is kept distinct from full-language source validation and
exact fallback.  The independent audit exhausted all orientations through
\(q=8\), checked every weighted transcript prefix, and ran four \(q=1024\)
both-label switching controls: 8,710 assertion-accounted checks, zero failures.

ADAPTIVEPROOFRACE83 is retained as a charged finite-portfolio upper theorem,
not all-decider coverage.  QUIET-EQUIV67 supplies the unit-free SCC quotient;
ADAPTIVE-COVER75 supplies the unit-action precursor and the no-sum warning.
The weighted theorem reconciles them without strengthening any restricted
query result into unrestricted deterministic time.  See
\path{research/main/2026-10-24_0648_MAIN_WEIGHTED_TRANSCRIPT_COVER108.tex},
\path{experiments/review/weighted_transcript_cover108_audit.py}, and
\path{results/raw/review/WEIGHTED_TRANSCRIPT_COVER108_raw.json}.

\paragraph{Single next target.}
For one fixed NP-complete encoding, work after complete source acquisition.
Define a representation-neutral trajectory object independently of observed
runtime, and prove for every total uniform exact adaptive decider that either
paid discovery, recognition and switching already exceed every polynomial,
or a reachable state has unavoidable superpolynomial continuation.  The
statement must survive SCC equivalence, propagation, Horn and affine rules,
learning, backtracking, restarts, component decomposition, memoization,
caching, recomputation, rereading, compact alternate representations,
SAT/UNSAT asymmetry and direct decision.  A fixed scalar, finite policy menu,
or sum of residual source potentials is not all-decider coverage.  The
post-acquisition bridge and \(P\ne NP\) remain \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{CERTIFICATE-PRODUCT-CEILING109: exact adaptive query ceiling and heterogeneous-switch audit.}
For every total Boolean label function on \(M\) explicit source fields,
deterministic adaptive query depth obeys the known certificate-product
bound sharpened by the trivial full-source scan,
\[
D(f)\le \min\{M,C_0(f)C_1(f)\}.
\]
Role~3's grid family is extremal:
\(C_0=C_1=q\) and \(D=q^2=M\).  Thus its quadratic pointwise-certificate
versus minimax gap is genuine but is exactly complete source acquisition;
the proper-3CNF image supplies only
\(\Omega(N/\log N)\) addressed sign-group reads and no positive
post-acquisition continuation bound.  Independent exhaustive audit covered
all 65,812 Boolean functions on one through four fields, 131,634
certificate/source assertions, exact \(q=2,3\) grid measures, 4,452
heterogeneous label checks, and four \(q=64\) SAT/UNSAT adaptive controls,
with zero failures.

Independent symbolic review of Role~4 HETEROGENEOUS-GATE72 confirms the
functional gate \(t=X\lor\ell_1\), the exact label
\[
D_{q,\beta,b}\in SAT\iff(\oplus_i\beta_i=0)\lor(b\ne1^q),
\]
counts \(n=11q+9,m_2=13q+5,k=9q+4,M=22q+9\), the
\(q/2\) disjoint-clause raw strong-2CNF backdoor lower bound, and the
Schaefer NP-complete endpoint \(\{OR_2,E_3\}\).  The family itself has a
charged linear SCC--affine--2SAT hybrid and direct sign scan, so it is a
constructive adaptive bypass rather than hardness amplification.

The exact spine is now: opposite-label explicit fields imply charged
acquisition up to \(M\) (\textbf{PROVED}); acquisition does not imply
expensive post-read continuation (\textbf{OPEN}); and no all-decider
superpolynomial conclusion follows (\textbf{OPEN}).  The next missing lemma

compatibility-or-continuation dichotomy for one fixed NP-complete encoding,

\paragraph{RESTART-RACE-CUTFLOW110: max-space SAT racing and the local-head boundary.}
Role~3's SAT-side race admits a serial geometric-restart implementation.
If \(t\) is the faster SAT-child runtime, charged guest work is \(<8t\).
Only one guest configuration is live, so peak guest space is the maximum
truncated child space plus \(O(\log t)\), rather than the sum of simultaneous
states.  Across SAT self-reduction stages this preserves
\(O_A(nM+nT_A^+(M))\) witness time while charging child construction, failed
work, restarts, prefix retention and verification.  The compatible transport
remainder is
\[
T_A^+(M)\ge\frac{L_{\rm wit}(F)-c_A nM}{c_A(n+1)};
\]
without a separately proved positive witness-search remainder it gives no
decision lower bound.

Independent review validates CUTFLOW85's
\(\Omega(k+k^2/(s+1))\) theorem only in its declared one-head local-source
model.  Addressed access or interleaving the same equality blocks gives an

\paragraph{AFFINE-EXCESS-NULLITY111: clustered excess-nullity interpolation.}
For an explicit mixed source \(E\wedge B\), with \(E\) affine over
\(\mathbb F_2\) and \(B\) unit/binary CNF, let affine component \(i\)
have nullity \(d_i\) and excess \(e_i=\max(d_i-1,0)\).  Build the
interaction graph whose edges are cross-component clauses of \(B\).
For each interaction cluster \(C_j\), put
\[
K_j=\sum_{i\in C_j}e_i.
\]
Charged Gaussian elimination, enumeration of the \(K_j\) excess basis bits,
and SCC on the residual 2-CNF give the exact upper bound
\[
P_{\rm parse+GE}(N)+\sum_j2^{K_j}P_{\rm sub+SCC}(N_j)+P_{\rm replay}(N),
\]
with polynomial peak space under serial depth-first execution.  Fixing
\(d_i-1\) basis bits leaves each source variable constant, the surviving
component bit, or its negation.  Interaction clusters factor, so their costs
add rather than multiply.  Thus \(\max_jK_j=O(\log N)\) is polynomial even
when total excess is linear.  Independent nullity-two SAT/UNSAT controls have
total \(K=q\) but profile \((1,\ldots,1)\) and \(O(q)\) local work; therefore
total excess nullity as a mandatory \(2^{\Omega(K)}\) cost is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.  The connected odd-XOR3/NAND graph-colouring
endpoint remains NP-complete and receives only a \(2^{|V|}\operatorname{poly}(N)\)
\emph{upper} bound.  A lower bound covering every state-adaptive exact decider
remains \OPEN{}.  The symbolic proof, 593-check audit (zero failures), raw
ledger and handoff are at
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1048_LSPV-5_AFFINE_EXCESS_NULLITY111.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix files.  \(P\ne NP\) remains \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{SIL-PROFILE-FIREWALL112: exact projection/profile instability.}
For \(q\) disjoint odd-XOR3 affine blocks, add a private variable \(z\) and
\[
 W_q(z)=\bigwedge_{i<q}\bigl[(z\vee a_i)\land(z\vee\neg a_i)\bigr].
\]
Each pair is SIL-RC \texttt{CP2} and is equivalent to \(z\), so the exact
trace
\[
 \texttt{CP2}^{q}\ \longrightarrow\ \texttt{CL3}^{q-1}\
 \longrightarrow\ \texttt{VE1}
\]
proves \(\exists z\,[F_q\land W_q(z)]\equiv F_q\), preserves the visible
relation, and gives every satisfying visible assignment exactly one \(z\)
extension.  Before the trace, the affine-block interaction graph is a
connected tree whose excess profile is \((q)\); after it, the exact same
visible relation has \(q\) components with profile \((1,\ldots,1)\).
Both fixed-metadata SAT/UNSAT controls are decided in linear word time by
this SIL rewrite.  Independently, TREEJOIN79 solves the unreduced tree with
local affine dimension \(h=2\) and state-pair ceiling \(2^{2h}=16\).
Thus raw cluster profile or connectedness as mandatory
\(2^{\Omega(K)}\) work is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}; it remains a valid
restricted-policy upper parameter only.  The adaptive ledger charges rule
recognition, \texttt{CP2}/\texttt{CL3}/\texttt{VE1} applications, switching,
SAT witness replay, UNSAT conflict detection, source access, and peak live
space.  The independent audit made 112 assertion-accounted checks with zero
failures, including exhaustive model counts through \(q=4\) and structural
controls through \(q=4096\).  The exact report and adjacent handoff, auditor,
and raw ledger are
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1148_LSPV-5_SIL_PROFILE_FIREWALL112.tex}.
A semantic compatibility obstruction invariant under affordable exact SIL
rewrites, tree joins, affine/SCC/Horn procedures, adaptive learning and
direct decision remains \textbf{OPEN}; the all-decider acquisition-or-
continuation theorem and \(P\ne NP\) remain \OPEN{}.



\paragraph{FIXED-SYNDROME-FIREWALL113: the complete fixed-syndrome rung is polynomial.}
For explicit 2-CNF \(B\) and \(Ax=b\) of \(\operatorname{GF}(2)\)-rank
\(\rho\), let \(G=\mathbb F_2^\rho\), \(g_j(0)=0\), and
\(g_j(1)=A_{*j}\).  Then \(\sum_j g_j(x_j)=b\) if and only if
\(Ax=b\).  Brakensiek--Gopi--Guruswami Proposition~5.2 gives
\((n+m_2)^{O(\rho)}\) deterministic exact time for this weighted
2-SAT-MOD state.  Hence every fixed-\(\rho\) rung, including the whole
two-check language, is in P; \(\rho=O(\log N)\) has an
\(N^{O(\log N)}\) upper bound.  This is a \textbf{KNOWN THEOREM} plus
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT} model mapping, not an FPT claim or a time lower
bound.  Independent audit of DUAL-PARITY-JOINT77 verified its exact
counts, syndrome, sensitivity, and \(h_2\) with 646 checks and zero
failures.  Fixed check count, marginal feasibility, affine hull, raw
backdoor size, and rank as mandatory work are therefore
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED} amplification mechanisms.  Unrestricted
rank remains NP-complete by independent selector rows, but the required
representation-neutral interaction lower bound for every adaptive
decider remains \OPEN{}.  Full proof and adaptive ledger:
\url{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1248_LSPV-5_FIXED_SYNDROME_FIREWALL113.tex};
handoff, executable audit, and raw JSON are adjacent.



\paragraph{EXISTENTIAL-CLOSURE-ENDPOINT114: existential closure is the exact ARC/FLMV endpoint obligation.}
Let \(\mathcal E\) be the pairs \((t,s)\) jointly achieved by one fixed,
uniform, total, all-input exact SAT decider in the FLMV random-access model.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT:} if \(P=NP\Rightarrow\mathcal E\ne\varnothing\) and
every \((t,s)\in\mathcal E\) has some same-realization successor in
\(\mathcal E\) with
\[
 t'\le\max\{11/10,3/5+t/2\},\qquad
 s'\le\max\{1/2,s/2\},
\]
then finite existential iteration contradicts FLMV.  Indeed
\(t_r\le6/5+2^{-r}(t_0-6/5)_+\) and
\(s_r\le\max\{1/2,2^{-r}s_0\}\), so a finite \(r\) gives
\(t_r<241/200\), \(s_r\le1/2\).  At \(c=241/200\),
\[
 d(c)=11642479/23232400
 =1/2+26279/23232400>1/2,
\]
and strict time slack absorbs the FLMV polylogarithmic transfer.

Thus a computable compiler, exponent discovery, universal selector, and
one invariant program identity across iterations are
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as logical necessities}; they remain obligations
only for proposed proofs that use them.  Each pair still requires one
uniform all-input machine with both bounds, and no restricted-family
contractor, separate time/space witnesses, nonuniform advice, fallback, or
mandatory witness/DNNF/proof output proves closure.  The all-input SAT
closure theorem itself remains \OPEN{}, so \(P\) versus \(NP\) remains
\OPEN{}.  Verified report, additive gap/coverage handoff, exact-rational
auditor, and raw ledger:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1248_LSPV-5_EXISTENTIAL_CLOSURE_ENDPOINT114.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.



\paragraph{COFINAL-QUANTIFIER-ENDPOINT116 (24 October 2026): exact cofinal quantifier repair and endpoint red team.}
Let
\[
 \mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}=
 \{(t,s):\exists c>\max\{1,t\},\
 s<\tfrac12((c+2)/c^2-c)\}.
\]
Primary FLMV implies \(\mathcal E\cap\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}=\varnothing\),
and this curved region is coordinatewise downward closed.  For every
\emph{fixed} unbounded \(U\subseteq\mathbb N\), the conditional assertion
\[
 \forall K\in U,\quad (K,K)\in\mathcal E\Longrightarrow
 \text{a finite route through \(\mathcal E\) reaches \(\mathcal B_{\rm FLMV}\)}
\]
is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT equivalent} to \(\mathcal E=\varnothing\), hence to
\(P\ne NP\).  It is vacuous when \(\mathcal E\) is empty; otherwise upward
closure supplies a sufficiently large diagonal in \(U\), and the asserted
route contradicts endpoint disjointness.  Thus cofinal routes are more
modular implementation targets than a universal compiler, but the abstract
conditional statement is not logically weaker than separation.

A literal alternative asserting the existence of an unbounded set of
\emph{already achievable} diagonals with such routes is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}: it is false when \(\mathcal E\) is empty because
no such set exists, and false when \(\mathcal E\) is nonempty because no
achievable route can enter the forbidden region.  The old unrestricted
\(t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)\) endpoint remains retired: the Williams primary statement
uses \(N^{o(1)}\) space.  Exact-rational regression passed eleven checks,
including two points in the curved FLMV region outside the reference box.
No reviewed UWI, selector, DNNF/proof-system, wrapper, hash/ratchet,
source-channel, low-width or balance-header result supplies an all-input
same-realization achievable-pair edge.  That edge or a fully charged cofinal
finite route remains \textbf{OPEN}; P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
Report and additive handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1348_LSPV-5_COFINAL_QUANTIFIER_ENDPOINT116.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.


\paragraph{WRITE-SHADOW-ENDPOINT118 (24 October 2026): exact log-word-RAM time--write pullback and zero-write sensitivity control.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in a declared restricted model; independent review pending.}
For one fixed uniform total all-input exact SAT decider on a blank-memory
$O(\log N)$-word RAM, count every mutable work-memory write, including cache
and auxiliary-table updates.  If its joint worst-case exponents are
$T(N)=N^{t+o(1)}$ and $W(N)+1=N^{u+o(1)}$, a balanced sparse dictionary
simulates it by one fixed FLMV random-access Turing machine in
$N^{t+o(1)}$ time and $N^{u+o(1)}$ work space.  Hence the RAM pair is
forbidden whenever
\[
 \exists c>\max\{1,t\}:\quad
 u<\beta(c),\qquad
 \beta(c)=\tfrac12\bigl((c+2)/c^2-c\bigr).
\]
In particular $(6/5,1/2)$ is forbidden using $c=241/200$ and
$\beta(c)=11642479/23232400>1/2$.  This is only the inverse image of the
published FLMV endpoint, not a new endpoint or ARC successor; bounded words,
blank memory, and charging every mutable channel are load-bearing.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED as a universal bridge:} necessary source reads force
work-memory writes.  A fixed-template parity-chain CNF has all $q$ orientation
fields label-sensitive, while a streaming finite-control policy uses $q$
input reads and zero work-memory writes on both SAT and UNSAT instances.
An exhaustive finite audit checked 22,621 sparse-memory traces and all 8,190
parity vectors for $1\le q\le12$, totaling 417,023 assertion-accounted checks
with zero failures.  Entropy reconstruction and circuit size/depth/layout
regions remain \textbf{PARTIAL/RESTRICTED}: a one-bit decider need not emit the
reconstructed object or instantiate the circuit/layout resource.
The first load-bearing implication remains \textbf{OPEN}: construct an
all-input charged route from cofinally many achievable seeds into the curved
FLMV region (equivalently into this time--write shadow for the declared RAM
implementation).  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\paragraph{RESOURCE-SCHEDULE-BRIDGE: exact online schedule accounting (15:00 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT under the explicit online-generator contract:}
if a fixed uniform bounded-fan-in controller uses \(A_T(N)\) generation
work, emits \(L(N)\) evaluation/source-access commands, retains at most

 T=\widetilde O(A_T+L+1),\qquad

\]
Hence exponents \(\gamma,\ell,\sigma,p\) enter the verified FLMV test only

\(\max\{\sigma,p\}<\beta(c)\).  This is a receiving bridge, not an all-input

and serial schedules, so raw gate count does not determine workspace.

\paragraph{QUIESCENT-CHILD-REUSE: scoped Gray-patch optimization (15:30 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for a valid supplied scoped-CNF event stream, but
PARTIAL/RESTRICTED as ARC:} materialize a block body and FORCE gadgets once,
then traverse union-boundary assignments in Gray order while changing four

\[
 T\le\widetilde O(N+Q+QT_A(L_{\max})),\qquad
 S\le\widetilde O(b+2^\omega+S_A(L_{\max})),
 \qquad W_{\rm gen}\le\widetilde O(N+Q).
\]
The 19,616-check audit covered 400 instances (386 SAT, 14 UNSAT) and matched

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} treating the four literal-field patches or

\paragraph{BIT-LEDGER-CEILING120: complete-state potential ceiling (15:48 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared elementary bit-operation model:}
for a fixed input/program with complete writable state in \(\{0,1\}^B\),
every globally \(L\)-Hamming-Lipschitz potential has oscillation at most
\(LB\).  Therefore endpoint gap divided only by the maximum per-one-bit
potential change certifies at most \(B\) state-changing steps.  This limits
that proof template; it is not a running-time upper bound and does not rule
out long legal paths.  A repair needs an independently proved legal-transition
bottleneck/long-path theorem, large representation, or charged history.
The vector ledger separately records primitive work, source/work reads,
changed and unchanged writes, complete-state flips, peak live bits,
cumulative bit-time and representation size.  Streaming parity controls
separate \(q\) source reads from zero versus one accumulator flip; addresses
and control still contribute complete-state flips.  Active-gate circuit
energy is not temporal flipping and must retain hardware size.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 13,979,869 assertions passed: directed Hamming-cube
edges through ten state bits, all three-bit action traces of length at most
five, Gray transitions through boundary width 16, both parity labels through
\(q=4096\), and exact FLMV rational arithmetic.  \textbf{OPEN:} a uniform

\paragraph{TYPED-LEGAL-POTENTIAL123: corrected flip channels and exact legal-graph escape (16:48 KST).}
Role~1's peer audit found a real type error in BIT-LEDGER-CEILING120:
if \(W_{\rm work}\) counts only work-memory writes, it cannot dominate
head, address or control flips.  The corrected \textbf{PROVED/EXACT}
ledger is
\[
 \Delta_{\rm work}\le W_{\rm work}\le U,\qquad
 \Delta_{\rm total}=\Delta_{\rm work}+\Delta_{\rm head}
 +\Delta_{\rm addr}+\Delta_{\rm ctrl}+\Delta_{\rm other}.
\]
Under one-bit complete-state events, \(\Delta_{\rm total}\le U\); no finite
\(g\) gives \(\Delta_{\rm total}\le g(W_{\rm work})\) for arbitrary
read-only scanners.  The erroneous untyped display is
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.  BIT-LEDGER-CEILING120's independent global
Hamming theorem \(\operatorname{osc}(\Phi)\le LB\) remains valid.

\textbf{PROVED/EXACT legal-graph repair:} if \(\Phi\) changes by at most
\(L\) on every legal transition edge, then
\[
 |\Phi(u)-\Phi(v)|\le Ld_G(u,v),\qquad
 \operatorname{osc}(\Phi)\le L\operatorname{diam}(G).
\]
A deterministic halting trajectory using at most \(B\) complete writable
bits is simple and has at most \(2^B-1\) steps.  Legal graph distance can
therefore be exponential in \(B\), escaping the global Hamming diameter.
But the path rank \(\Phi(C_i)=i\) is defined from the trajectory itself:
without an independent structural definition and endpoint-gap proof it is
the circular remaining-runtime potential in different notation.  A Gray
counter gives an exponentially long optional path with small state; another
SAT decider may omit it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 1,245,297 assertions passed, covering 131,054
reflected-Gray legal steps, 983,041 Hamming-cube edges through 16 state
bits, binary-counter flip totals and 26 typed SAT/UNSAT scanner controls.
The audit accepts Role~2's source-dependent raw-child/all-witness cover
bounds and Role~3's grouped one-way complete-witness bit-time bound only as
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}: affine/direct decision, interleaved
generation, random-access regeneration and root-label shortcuts bypass the
respective service contracts.  Role~4's cyclic arithmetic bit service is
also accepted as \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}; it
\textbf{FALSIFIES/RETIRES} universal selector-array and \(QK\)
materialization-write floors, while MINIMAL-UNSAT-PROJECTION20 still blocks
status-only parent gluing.

The all-input same-machine ARC successor and every noncircular all-decider
legal-path invariant remain \OPEN{}.  Report, executable, raw result and

\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1648_LSPV-5_TYPED_LEGAL_POTENTIAL123.tex}

\subsection*{PINNED-CHILD-SEPARATOR124: deletion collision is not pin-stable}

The MINIMAL-UNSAT-PROJECTION20 pair still proves that every strict
clause-deletion child is SAT on both opposite-label parents.  The stronger
suggestion that source-independent pinned children also collide is now
\textbf{FALSIFIED}.  In the exact ordering used by Role~4, the only changed
clause has zero-based index \(d=M-4\).  Add three ordinary proper-3CNF unit
blocks pinning
\[
        x_m=1,\qquad a_m=0,\qquad b_m=0
\]
to each cyclic \(K\)-clause child.  Then
\[
 j_*=\left\lfloor\frac{M-4}{K}\right\rfloor
\]
is the first cyclic window whose complete pinned-child encodings can differ.
All earlier encodings are byte-identical.  At \(j_*\), the \(F_m\) child is
UNSAT because its clause
\((\neg x_m\vee a_m\vee b_m)\) is false, whereas the \(G_m\) child is SAT
under the restriction of its known global assignment.  The three pin blocks
add 12 clauses and six variables, so the ordinary child remains
\(N^{1/2+o(1)}\) bits.  This is \textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted}.

This refinement does not construct parent gluing.  A SAT parent can have an
incompatible UNSAT pinned child, and an UNSAT parent can have a SAT selected
child.  A promise controller can jump directly to \(j_*\); after canonical
recognition it can decide the pair still more cheaply by reading the one
changed source field.  Thus neither the matched pair nor the pinned query
forces expensive acquisition or post-acquisition work.

The newest Role-4 PERIODIC-CHILD-COLLISION83 correction is accepted:
distinct cyclic index selections need not yield distinct serialized child
contents.  Its opposite-label periodic parents have identical vectors of
isolated child model counts and only \(1+8K\) versus \(1+7K\) distinct child
contents.  This retires unqualified child-content uniqueness and
status/count-only gluing, while leaving the all-distinct-record flat-buffer
theorem and virtual bit service intact.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the new standard-library audit completed 7,375
assertion-accounted checks with zero failures for
\(M=16,32,64,128,256,1024,4096\), including every unpinned child, every
pinned prefix through \(j_*\), two unrestricted promise escapes, and
SAT/UNSAT canonical-field, Horn, SCC, affine and DPLL controls.
Report, executable, raw data and coverage handoff are at
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_1748_LSPV-5_PINNED_CHILD_SEPARATOR124.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.

The load-bearing theorem remains \OPEN{}: a total uniform all-input
source-consistent compatibility rule for ordinary pinned/interface children
whose fully charged recursion, failed trials, caches and live ancestors yield
a favorable simultaneous achievable pair, or a cofinal finite route into the
verified FLMV box.  The next hostile control is coherent shared-boundary
pinning on Role~4's periodic opposite-label same-count pair through two
recursive levels.  ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain \OPEN{}.


\paragraph{NAMED-PROFILE-IDENTITY125 (independent synthesis, 24 October 2026).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the declared named-profile/CSP model.}
For an ordered proper clause \(C_i\), retain its ordered source-variable
triple \(V_i\) and complete eight-bit local truth profile \(p_i\).
The unique zero row of \(p_i\) reconstructs all three literal signs, so
\[
 \nu(F)=(n,m,(V_i,p_i)_{i=1}^m)
\]
uniformly reconstructs the original ordered 3CNF.  Its exact gluing rule is
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(F)\iff
 \exists a\in\{0,1\}^n\ \forall i,\quad a|_{V_i}\in
 \{b:p_i(b)=1\},
\]
which is literally the original Boolean CSP join.  Thus the current local
profile route has a sharp dichotomy: discarding \(V_i\) gives Role~3's
connected opposite-label collision; retaining \(V_i\) restores exactness but
does not contract compatibility.

A complete second profile level is information-idempotent.  The named
profiles of all 13 clauses in the canonical pin child \(H(C,b)\) reconstruct
that child, while the child is already a fixed function of the first clause
profile and pin row \(b\).  Recursive enumeration is optional transcription,
not a source of global shared-variable information.  This independently
narrows PIN-PROFILE-JOIN-FIREWALL89 and closes the proposed two-level
source-name test unless a separate all-input theorem forces acyclic or
bounded-width compatibility.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 9,131 deterministic checks, including 64 first-level
pin children, 64 complete two-level reconstructions, 6,656 independently
solved grandchild statuses, Role~3's connected pair, Role~4's periodic
same-count pair, the minimal-UNSAT pair, and Horn/2SAT/affine/decide-first
SAT/UNSAT controls; zero failures.  Source report, code, raw data and

The first missing theorem is still an all-input same-realization construction

\subsection*{BOUNDARY-ORACLE-FACTOR128: root-hard support geometry has a one-bit quotient}

For every proper 3CNF \(F\) and every explicit Boolean circuit
\(C:\{0,1\}^d\to\{0,1\}\) with nonempty support, a uniform proper-3CNF
construction \(H(F,C)\) has
\[
 \operatorname{SAT}(H(F,C)[b])=C(b)\wedge\operatorname{SAT}(F),
 \qquad
 \operatorname{SAT}(H(F,C))=\operatorname{SAT}(F).
\]
A functional proper Tseitin encoding forces the output of \(C\) to one, and
each source clause is guarded through a fresh two-clause split.  The formula
has \(n+d+O(|C|)+m\) variables and \(2m+O(|C|)\) clauses.  This
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} theorem independently accepts Role~1's
root-hard threshold case and extends it to singleton, co-singleton, subcube,
parity, disconnected and other succinct supports.

For any deterministic adaptive boundary controller, evaluate \(C(b)\) at
each requested word.  Unsupported words return zero.  The first supported
word reads \(\operatorname{SAT}(F)\), caches that bit, and every later answer
is computed locally.  Induction gives the identical transcript with at most
one source-label call.  This does not decide the source bit cheaply.  It
shows that exponential pinned multiplicity and support geometry do not by
themselves create independent computations.  An ordinary supported child
retains \(N^{1+o(1)}\) length, so the transport scale is \(\lambda=1\).

The audit covered 310 source formulas, 1,860 constructions, 17,520 exhaustive
boundary words and 9,300 adaptive transcripts containing 203,100 queries:
301,304 assertion-accounted checks, zero failures.  Affine/XOR, Horn,
dual-Horn, padded 2SAT, independent-component, SAT and UNSAT controls were
included.  This finite evidence is \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}.  The report,
reproducer, raw summary and handoff are
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-24_2148_LSPV-5_BOUNDARY_ORACLE_FACTOR128.tex},
\path{experiments/boundary_oracle_factor128.py},
\path{results/raw/2026-10-24_LSPV5_BOUNDARY_ORACLE_FACTOR128_summary.json},
and the adjacent handoff.

LAZY-PIN-SOURCE93 is accepted in its direct-copy calculus: source-indexed
regeneration saves a full child buffer but the complete ordinary child grows.
PAD-FLOOR-SCOPED87 is accepted only for valid closed scoped streams and cannot
shrink this continuation.  TREE-AFFINE-JOIN122 is accepted as a constructive
restricted upper bound.  The concurrent SCOPE-CLIQUE-FIREWALL88 proof is
accepted in its clause/name-preserving scoped-stream model: interval Helly
gives exact minimum width \(p+1\), fixed-polynomial disjoint padding cannot
make \(w=o(\log N')\), and a paid source recognizer decides both matched
labels with logarithmic writable controller bits on the recognized family.
Thus padding/reordering alone cannot repair arbitrary width, while large
syntactic scope width is not a machine-space lower bound.  The original
HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 executable remains unrecovered.  The all-input same-machine favorable successor, cofinal finite
route into the verified FLMV box, and P versus NP remain \OPEN{}.


\subsection*{MULTIBIT-ENDPOINT129 update (LSPV-5)}

Independent review accepts Role~3's guarded-trie identities
\[
 \SAT(H[z=i])=\SAT(F_i),\qquad \SAT(H)=\bigvee_i\SAT(F_i),
\]
the full \(2^k\) matched pinned profile, exact size algebra, and its
same-machine promise recurrence
\[
 (t,s)\longmapsto
 \left(\max\{1,\tfrac12+\tfrac t2\},
       \max\{\tfrac12,\tfrac s2\}\right).
\]
Starting from a fixed diagonal pair \((K,K)\), exact iterates are
\(t_j=1+(K-1)/2^j\) and
\(s_j=\max\{1/2,K/2^j\}\); therefore, \emph{if} this were an all-input
achievable-pair edge, finitely many fixed steps would enter the verified FLMV
box \(t<241/200\),
\(s<11642479/23232400\).  This is strict finite-entry arithmetic, not the
missing SAT theorem.

The construction remains a recognized promise.  Its natural arbitrary-source
embedding has quadratic stretch and gives only
\(T_F(N_F)\le N_F^{\max\{2,1+t\}+o(1)}\); an all-input fallback retains the
old exponent.  Thus independent semantic bits plus genuinely short ordinary
children do not by themselves fill ARC-EDGE-90.  An all-input, both-label,

\subsection*{THREE-STATE-JOIN-FIREWALL130 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
Independent review accepts Role~3's EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95:
positive exact-one SAT compiles linearly into disjoint modules with exactly
three explicit local signatures, affine-hull dimension two, and equality-only
source-name links.  For \(m\) modules and
\(L=3m-n_+\) equality links, the ordinary proper-3CNF compiler has
\(n'=4m+2L\) variables and \(m'=6m+4L\) clauses.

The matched four-module positive source is UNSAT, while changing the last
module to \(E(\neg b,c,d)\) is SAT.  Both compile with
\((n',m',N')=(32,56,2938)\), identical unsigned supports and interaction
edges, and complete binary arc consistency leaves all three signatures at all
four modules.  Independent testing made 1,871 assertion-accounted checks with
zero discrepancies, including 240 randomized signed sources.  Exhausting all
eight signs on the last module supplies an important scope refinement:
arc consistency solves several variants; the selected opposite-label pair is
the deliberately matched no-pruning firewall, not a generic claim.

Thus local support cardinality, local affine rank, feasibility and pairwise
arc consistency do not alone decide global compatibility.  The result is not
a runtime lower bound: the matched templates are source-recognizable, bounded
treewidth remains an algorithmic upper-bound parameter, and raw cycle count
has easy affine/nonaffine controls.  It complements rather than completes
MULTIBIT-ENDPOINT129: the latter has genuinely square-root ordinary children
but only a promise OR join; the former has linear exact NP-complete gluing but
no power-short child service or improved all-input simultaneous pair.  The
same-machine charged successor remains \OPEN{}.

\section{STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131 update}
The named four-module exact-one pair now gives a sharper
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} compatibility firewall.  Let
\[
 U=E(a,b,c)\wedge E(a,b,d)\wedge E(a,c,d)\wedge E(b,c,d)
\]
and let \(S\) change only the last module to \(E(\neg b,c,d)\).
Then \(U\) is UNSAT, \(S\) is SAT, but every proper subset of their
four modules is SAT.  Hence the complete 15-bit proper-subjoin SAT-status
vector and every adaptive transcript restricted to those bits are all YES
on both labels; all 14 nontrivial partition block-status vectors collide as
well.  Exact named half-relations remain sufficient:
their intersections have sizes zero and one, respectively.  Exact counts
are also richer on this pair (the subjoin \(\{1,4\}\) has three versus two
models), and one source sign field distinguishes the promise inputs.
Therefore this is a status-only semantic no-go, not a post-acquisition or
unrestricted time lower bound.

The result combines the two previously separate restricted ingredients:
MULTIBIT-ENDPOINT129 has power-short children but an easy OR join, while
THREE-STATE-JOIN-FIREWALL130 has globally hard named compatibility but no
power-short service.  A valid all-input successor must encode compatibility
into genuinely sublinear ordinary child labels or carry and charge a richer
global join.  Arbitrary transformed children are not ruled out.  The
standard-library audit recorded 9,562 assertion groups with zero final
discrepancies, including all strict subjoins, partitions, renamings, module
orders and hostile signings.  The two-negation controls correctly show that
the strict-subjoin property is not invariant under every signing.

The newest specialist results remain restricted and are accepted at their
stated strength: SIGNED-DEGREE2-MATCH133 is a constructive matching/FPT
upper bound; LINEAR-SPACE-CUT-REPLAY127 removes optional predecessor storage
for certified scoped streams; SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96 proves that local
width without global name coherence is unsound and charges equality repair;
PARITY-MARGINAL-COUNT92 proves strict-pin count blindness on a different
affine family whose ordinary children remain source-scale.  None supplies
an all-input same-machine achievable-pair successor.

\section{NAMED-FRONTIER-COMPOSITION132 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL two-level audit;
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
Let \(U\) be the four positive exact-one triples on
\(\{a,b,c,d\}\), and let \(V\) negate \(b\) only in the
\(\{b,c,d\}\) triple.  Every proper module subjoin is SAT on both
sources, while \(U\) is UNSAT and \(V\) has the unique source solution
\((1,0,0,0)\).  Thus any proper-block SAT-status summary collides.
For a supplied valid closed scoped event stream, retaining complete
assignments to each set \(S_i\) of live \emph{original source names}
repairs correctness: adjacent block witnesses glue exactly, and the number
of compatible boundary pairs on edge \((i,j)\) is
\[
  2^{|S_i\cup S_j|},
\]
not the product proxy \(2^{|S_i|+|S_j|}\).  This is an exact restricted
algorithmic identity, not mandatory work for arbitrary SAT deciders.

An independent deterministic implementation compiled each exact-one module
to five proper ternary clauses, created fully serialized pin children, and
ran fixed, union-optimal and product-optimal schedules for two recursive
levels.  All six source/schedule combinations returned the correct labels.
The 1,078-bit sources have width four.  Exact union scheduling reduced
generated child volume from 19,180,802 to 1,702,762 bits on the UNSAT source
and from 19,284,358 to 1,836,548 bits on the SAT source, but the largest
child remained 2,808 bits, larger than the source.  Eighty-two assertion
groups, including exact-one truth rows and brute compatible-pair counts,
had zero failures.  The even/odd K$_4$ Tseitin controls were decided directly
by paid affine parity, bypassing frontier enumeration.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} strict-subjoin SAT bits suffice; a large named
frontier forces arbitrary-decider work; or this finite overlap optimization
already proves ordinary-child contraction.  \textbf{OPEN:} a uniform
all-input exact successor jointly realizing a favorable time--space pair,
or a finite cofinal charged route into the verified FLMV box.
Verified report, executable, raw result and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-25_0150_LSPV-5_NAMED_FRONTIER_COMPOSITION132.tex}
and adjacent same-prefix artifacts.  P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.



\section{INDEXED-MERGE-POTENTIAL-CEILING133 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT post-acquisition telescoping ceiling;
COMPUTATIONAL restricted comparison; ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
For any fixed charged execution \(C_0,\ldots,C_T\), if an independently
defined terminal-zero potential satisfies
\[
 \Phi(C_i)-\Phi(C_{i+1})\le L(N)u_i
\]
on every legal transition, then exactly
\[
 \Phi(C_0)\le L(N)\sum_i u_i=L(N)W_{\rm post}.
\]
On the canonical balanced named-join family \(J_q(A,B)\), paid indexed
two-pointer merge has \(W_{\rm post}=O(q\log q)\) and \(O(\log N)\)
writable bits after full grammar acquisition.  Since its compatibility
matrix has rank \(\binom q{q/2}\ge2^q/(q+1)\), raw relation rank,
raw context count, and \(\binom q{q/2}{\bf1}[\mathrm{not\ halted}]\)
cannot be polynomial-\(L\) bounded-drop post-acquisition potentials.
The logarithm of rank is only \(\Theta(q)\) and is compatible with the
input-order upper bound.

A same-metadata finite test compared indexed merge with the supplied
one-level eager named-child policy on SAT complements, UNSAT identical
halves and UNSAT one-late-collision sources for \(q\le10\).
At \(q=10\), every source has \(N=2106\) bits.  The broad-frontier UNSAT
case used one comparison, at most 331 post-acquisition bit operations
and 82 live writable bits under the declared conservative schedule,
whereas the eager policy serialized 13,922 ordinary child bits and had
a 2,106-bit largest child.  This is a strategy cost, not mandatory SAT
work.  Role~3's \(\Omega(N)\) eager-pin child floor on expanding sources
remains valid for its fixed compiler; affine recognition and indexed
merge show why it does not quantify over every adaptive decider.

The full bit ledger charges acquisition, source/work reads, unchanged
writes, addressing, control, selection, representation bits, children,
cache and bit-time separately.  Sixty-five assertion groups had zero
failures.  Report, code, raw result and coverage handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-25_0246_LSPV-5_INDEXED_MERGE_POTENTIAL_CEILING133.tex},

\path{results/raw/review/INDEXED_MERGE_POTENTIAL_CEILING133_raw.json},
and the adjacent dated handoff.
The remaining obligation is an independently large potential surviving
every legal adaptive transition, or an actual all-input uniform jointly
favorable resource successor including recognition failure and fallback.
P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\section{M22-ANCHOR-NONADAPTER137 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT target and grammar lemmas; COMPUTATIONAL finite audit;
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP OPEN.}
Adopt the canonical target $\mathcal C_\star$: unique proper clausewise-monotone
ternary CNFs with exactly two occurrences of each sign per active variable,
excluding only formulas in which every clause has its sign-flipped complement.
The six-variable anchor with positive supports
$\{123,145,246,356\}$ and negative supports
$\{124,136,235,456\}$ is in $\mathcal C_\star$, has witness $111000$ and
exactly 20 models.  The piecewise paired-to-anchor reduction and the no-test
valid-source map $F\mapsto F\dot\cup Y$ preserve satisfiability and establish
the announced NP-completeness transport, not a time lower bound.

The independent red-team result is the exact recursive-type firewall
\[
 G\dot\cup Y\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}
 \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad G\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}
\]
for disjoint variables.  Thus $Y$ repairs the fully-paired exclusion only; it
cannot repair a pin child that has left the base grammar.  Setting $x_1=1$ in
$Y$ already leaves two binary clauses.  Appending another fresh $Y$ remains
outside $\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$, so a $\mathcal C_\star$-promised solver cannot
be called without a separate paid type-restoring compiler.

A complete six-variable enumeration found 75 balanced support families and
5625 ordered signed formulas.  Their defect distribution is
$q=0:75$, $q=2:1260$, $q=3:1680$, $q=4:2610$; all are SAT.  The published
198-variable, 264-clause D\"ocker fixture was independently syntax-checked as
strict M22, nonpaired, with 132 clauses of each sign and $q=132$; its UNSAT
label remains a known theorem, not a new brute-force result.  All 12 one-level
and 120 ordered two-level pin/reanchor tests failed target membership for the
predicted nonternary reason.

For a complete $d$-bit branch, $B=2^d$ children of residual size $k-d$ have
$\Psi=2^d2^{k-d}=2^k$, hence $R_{\rm search}=0$.  This rejects raw removed
variables as net exhaustive saving while preserving symbolic factor, affine,
Horn, SCC, learned and cached continuations as legitimate alternatives.
The bit ledger charges validation, source rereads, rule selection, failed type
restoration, every child/header write, virtual addressing, live buffers,
workspace traffic and bit-time across two levels.  It supplies neither an
all-input contraction nor an independently large post-acquisition potential.

\section{M22-RENAMING-REPAIR-COLLAPSE138 update (LSPV-5)}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted identity; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls;
general adapters, ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP OPEN.}
For $F\in\mathcal C_{\rm M22}$, pin an active $x$ to $b$, then apply the
canonical one-fresh-variable, support-faithful quota repair: fill each of the
two binary residual records by the same-sign fresh literal $z$, and restore
each of the two satisfied records with $x$ replaced by $z$.  Clause by clause,
the result is exactly the sign-preserving alpha-renaming
\[
 Q_{x,b,z}(F\mid x=b)=\rho_{x\mapsto z}(F).
\]
It is independent of $b$, restores the grammar and preserves model count and
paired/nonpaired status, but represents the unpinned parent rather than the
fixed branch.  Fixing $z=b$ recovers the branch only by recreating the
non-M22 residual.

After $d$ repaired pins, all $2^d$ nominal children are alpha-isomorphic
full-size parents.  Independent exhaustive completion has
$\Psi=2^d2^k=2^{k+d}$ and $R_{\rm search}=-d$; a cache can merge them to one
parent-equivalent child but cannot recover the fixed branch labels.  Thus this
repair gives no all-input simultaneous resource successor and no ARC edge.
The result is deliberately restricted to one-fresh-variable support-faithful
slot repair; general semantic gadgets remain \OPEN{}.

The exhaustive audit covered all 5,625 $k=6$ M22 formulas, all variables and
both bits (67,500 repair cases), with zero identity, grammar, model-count or
pair-status failures.  All 12 one-level and 120 ordered two-level $Y$ controls
strictly lost fixed-branch information.  The 198-variable D\"ocker fixture
passed 396 syntax/identity checks; its UNSAT label was imported, not
recomputed.  The two-level bit ledger separately charges parsing, source and
workspace traffic, unchanged writes, indexing/addressing, materialized or
virtual child access, representation switches, failed trials, cache
recognition, live ancestors/buffers and cumulative bit-time.  Materialization
writes $\Theta(N)$ ordinary child bits; virtualization stores aliases but
charges every subsequent rewritten source access.  Neither route shrinks the
semantic instance.

This also rejects nominal repaired-child count or table size as an
unavoidable post-acquisition potential: alpha-canonicalization merges the
representations.  No independent complete-state potential is proved.
Report, code, raw evidence and handoff:
\path{research/agents/lspv-5/2026-10-25_0450_LSPV-5_M22_RENAMING_REPAIR_COLLAPSE138.tex},

\paragraph{M22-ENFORCER-PORT-FIREWALL139: exact external-quota obstruction for D\"ocker's port-once enforcer (25 Oct 2026, 05:50 KST).}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT for D\"ocker's 133-clause monotone enforcer interface; COMPUTATIONAL exhaustive audits; broader adapter, ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP OPEN.}
The primary enforcer $S(v_1,v_2,v_3)$ uses each external port once, gives each of its 99 fresh variables signed degree $(2,2)$, and has 133 clauses.  Hence lifting a padded source clause preserves every external signed occurrence count exactly.  Pinning any variable of an M22 formula deletes its two true-sign clauses and shortens its two opposite-sign clauses.  Duplicating either surviving literal in each binary residual adds only opposite-sign occurrences, so at least one true-sign external quota deficit survives all four padding choices.  Reapplying the same enforcer preserves that deficit: the result is never in $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}$, hence never in $\mathcal C_\star$.  This retires only the exact port-once repair; an adapter that adds new externally incident clauses remains \OPEN{}.
Independent checks found zero externally balanced outputs over $Y$ (12 pins/48 padding choices), all 5,625 six-variable M22 formulas (67,500 pins/270,000 choices), and every pin of D\"ocker's 198-variable, 264-clause source instance (396 pins/1,584 choices).  The $Y$ model count 20 and witness 111000 were rechecked; the source instance's UNSAT label is imported.  With the declared dense layout, two lifts after one pin expand $Y$ from $(k,m,N)=(5,6,83)$ to $(599,798,26{,}366)$ and $(79{,}601,106{,}134,5{,}731{,}289)$; the source residual expands from $(197,262,7{,}100)$ to $(26{,}135,34{,}846,1{,}672{,}655)$ and $(3{,}475{,}889,4{,}634{,}518,319{,}781{,}810)$.  Materialization charges every header/literal write and peak output storage; a virtual accessor instead pays address decoding, template/source reads and control, but still exposes a non-target formula.  Optional expansion size and negative exhaustive $R_{\rm search}$ are not mandatory decision work and do not define a post-acquisition potential.  Direct recognition, affine/Horn/SCC/matching solvers, caches and alternate representations remain legal.

\paragraph{M22-PIN-DIVISIBILITY141 (24 October 2026): survivor-retaining normalizers cannot create power-smaller explicit ordinary children.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted syntax and source-injection theorem, independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls, no unrestricted SAT lower bound.}
Preserve the canonical clausewise-monotone ordinary \(3\)-CNF
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) with unique proper clauses and exactly
two occurrences per variable per polarity;
\(\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}\)
excludes \emph{only fully} complementary-clause-paired sources, not
partially paired inputs. Invalid encodings are rejected.
With \(k\) active original variables, \(m=4k/3\),
\(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\), any nonempty \(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\)
has \(k\in3\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\).
After pinning \(d\) distinct original names, an explicit ordinary
\(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{M22}}\) child \(G\) that retains \emph{every}
unpinned original name injectively as a distinct active name must satisfy
\[
 k(G)\ge\max\{6,3\lceil(k-d)/3\rceil\}
       =\max\{6,k-3\lfloor d/3\rfloor\},\qquad
 m(G)=4k(G)/3.
\]
For \(d=1,2\), the child has at least the parent's \(k\) names and
\(m\) clauses; for \(d=o(k)\), its explicit dense indexed bit length is
\(\Omega(N)\), not \(N^{\lambda+o(1)}\) with fixed \(\lambda<1\).
Two \emph{actual sequential} one-pin, survivor-retaining
normalizations likewise obey \(k_2\ge k_1\ge k_0\).
This is \emph{only} a grammar/injection floor; it neither enforces
conditional SAT/UNSAT branch semantics nor forbids semantic elimination,
alternative representations, or charged virtual access.
Role~5's earlier one-alias repair additionally loses pin semantics,
returning an alpha-renamed full parent. A raw Shannon pin exits the M22
grammar. Role~2's \(q=2\) four-color NAE witness and Role~3's
perfect-matching family remain positive solver controls.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 48 integer floor pairs, verified six-variable
\(Y\) with 20 models and connected \(Z_h\) controls, 12 single-pin
and 120 ordered two-pin raw descendants; 358 assertion-accounted
checks, zero discrepancies. No FLMV bit-work/peak-space, flip or
cumulative-bit-time measurement.
The exhaustive budget
\(\Psi=2^d2^{k-d}=2^k\), \(R_{\rm search}=0\), is optional and
does not force arbitrary decider work.
\textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN} for an all-input uniform jointly achieved
ordinary-SAT favorable route into
\(t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400\), with the historical
universal cosine landing \textbf{RETIRED}.
\textbf{DIRECT-POST OPEN} for an independent superpolynomial

\paragraph{M22-CONNECTED-CYCLIC-LIFT139 (25 October 2026): connected high-defect easy-island firewall.}

k=6h,\qquad m=8h,\qquad

and connectivity potential, \emph{not} a worst-case
