\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (GLOBALQUOTA80, external review pending).}
For every fixed valid proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ source, the two positive
signed-occurrence positions per original variable uniquely identify its
entire same-support signing.  Hence even against \emph{all} legal
multi-field sign perturbations, not merely the one-swap star,
the fixed-input SAT-label sign-probe certificate complexity is at most
$2n=3m/2=O(N/\log N)$.  This is not a uniform SAT decision-tree bound.

On $t$ disjoint octahedral eight-clause supports, there are
$6^{6t}$ valid balanced signings on $n=6t$ variables.
A fixed source needs exactly $2n$ probes for full sign identification,
while a uniform deterministic \emph{source-identification} decision tree
needs exactly $3n$ worst-case probes, via the exact adversarial
potential $\Phi=\sum_v\lceil\log_2|K_v^{\rm consistent}|\rceil$.
Yet \emph{all} these signings are SAT: two disjoint-support clause
failures overlap with probability $1/64$, strengthening the eight-clause
union bound to $63/64<1$.  Thus SAT-root decision on this sign-fiber
promise uses zero sign probes and a source-recognizing total exact
algorithm constructs a witness in $O(N/\log N)$ word time on the family.
At least $2^t$ different full satisfying relations occur despite the
constant SAT label.  The complete standard-library audit exhaustively
checked all $6^6=46{,}656$ one-block legal signings (zero UNSAT, model
counts $4$--$22$), with $118$ separate brute-force checks and
SAT/UNSAT symbolic hostile controls; status \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}
for finite observations.  The first missing implication remains an
all-decider superpolynomial \emph{post-acquisition} cost theorem, not an
input-query or rich-relation enumeration bound.  $P$ versus $NP$
remains \OPEN{}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, GAUGEFORK81 (independent review pending).}
Using LSPV-2's explicitly source-certified D\"ocker UNSAT source $U$
($n=198,m=264$) and its source-coherent two-sign-swap SAT neighbor $V$
(an independent 198-bit witness was replayed), take $t$ disjoint
$U/V$ blocks and independently complement all occurrences of any
source variable.  The $\mathbb Z_2^{198t}$ gauge action preserves the
proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ language and SAT labels.  The resulting
\emph{both-label} family contains exactly $2^{199t}$ distinct encodings
with $n=198t$, $m=264t$ and
$N=(1056t+2)\lceil\log_2(64(462t+1))\rceil$ bits; it is SAT exactly
when all $t$ blocks use $V$.  The quotient of legal four-occurrence
two-positive/two-negative sign patterns by whole-variable complementation
has exactly $3^n$ gauge classes, not $6^n$.

On every all-$V$ input, \emph{every} deterministic exact decider for
the full signed exact-$(2,2)$ language must inspect at least one
\emph{swapped} and one \emph{unchanged} sign field of $x_3$ in each
block.  The source-probe potential
$\Phi=2t-\sum_i \alpha_i$, with $\alpha_i$ the number of these two
types already queried in block $i$, decreases by at most one per
field read, and accepting correctly requires $\Phi=0$.  This proves
a sharp $2t$ distinct-field lower bound, attained by reading just
two $x_3$ sign fields per block and testing their XOR.  A uniform
full-source validator and template recognizer runs in
$O(N/\log N)$ word time, reverting to an exact general solver outside
the recognized promise. Thus exponentially many valid sources with
\emph{both labels} coexist with near-linear SAT decision.  The
restricted acquisition bound does \emph{not} imply a superlinear or
superpolynomial all-decider cost bridge.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} the actual 264-clause $U$ source, the sign
swap, the $V$ witness and all 64 projected-core rows were independently
checked, as were 390 deterministically gauged trials at
$t\in\{1,2,3\}$: $1{,}396{,}170$ recorded assertion checks,
zero failures.  The six local UNSAT projection certificates are an
explicit \emph{imported} ROLE-2 dependency, not freshly replayed.
\textbf{OPEN:} the all-uniform-decider superpolynomial post-acquisition
semantic compression/continuation theorem, and both $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$.  This supplements, rather than supersedes, the
GLOBALQUOTA80 sign-certificate bound.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINECUTDP82 (independent review pending):}
For every affine Boolean system $Ax=b$, every ordering of its source variables,
and every prefix/suffix cut $L\mid R$, let
$\eta=\operatorname{rank}A_L+\operatorname{rank}A_R-\operatorname{rank}A$.
If the system is consistent, the number of distinct complete suffix
residual functions induced by all prefix assignments is exactly
\[
 W=2^\eta+\mathbf1_{\{\operatorname{rank}A_L>\eta\}};
\]
for an inconsistent system $W=1$. The proof counts nonempty right-hand
sides in $(b+\operatorname{im}A_L)\cap\operatorname{im}A_R$ and the optional
constant-FALSE residual. All values are polynomial-time computable by
Gaussian elimination. The forward/reverse syndrome compatibility test
checks this same affine-space intersection in polynomial time
\emph{without enumerating} the potentially exponential residual set.

On CONNECTRANK71's connected signed exact-$(2,2)$ source $H_g$,
where $n=3g$, $m=4g$ and
$H_g\equiv\bigwedge_i(x_i\leftrightarrow y_i)$ with free $z_i$,
the ordered read-once dynamic-programming cut immediately after all
$x_i$ needs $2^g$ distinct residual states; interleaving
$x_i,y_i,z_i$ keeps the maximum at most three.
The shared-source NOT1/OR2/AND2 assignment-evaluation circuit has
$15g-1$ gates and $27g-2$ directed references, and existentially
eliminating its deterministic gate variables preserves exactly the
original source residual relation. Gate-variable Tseitin encoding need
not preserve signed exact-$(2,2)$ physical occurrence quotas.
Under an \emph{explicit residual-record} calculus, $J$ required cut
states cost at least $J$ append writes; this is a restricted
representation lower bound and \textbf{does not} force any arbitrary
one-bit SAT decider to build that table. Gaussian elimination, SCC,
source recognition, order selection, caching, and decide-first avoid it.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 12,285 seeded assertions, zero failures,
including 550 randomized affine systems and 2,829 random cut comparisons,
plus 4,680 original-CNF versus shared-circuit assignment checks.

\paragraph{M22-WITNESS-VOLUME142 (24 October 2026, 14:20 PDT): exact negative search savings for one particular unpruned one-sided witness enumerator.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only in the declared independently replayed raw-leaf protocol, with independent review pending; COMPUTATIONAL finite checks; ARC-EDGE-90, DIRECT-POST and P versus NP OPEN.}
For canonical clausewise-monotone exact-(2,2) \(\mathcal C_\star=\mathcal C_{\rm M22}\setminus\mathcal P_{\rm pair}\), write \(k\) for active original variables, \(m=4k/3\) for clauses and \(N=\Theta(k\log(k+2))\) for physical indexed bits.
With \(q=|P\setminus Q|\), choose one TRUE variable occurrence from each unmatched positive triple, let \(d_\sigma\) count distinct selected source variables, and \emph{only for the deliberately unpruned protocol that exhaustively revisits every raw residual assignment} put
\[
\Psi_{\rm raw}=\sum_{\sigma\in[3]^q}2^{k-d_\sigma}
\ge 3^q2^{k-q}=2^k(3/2)^q,\qquad
R_{\rm search}=k-\log_2\Psi_{\rm raw}\le-q\log_2(3/2)<0.
\]
Equality holds precisely when unmatched positive supports are pairwise disjoint. Exact double counting gives \(\Psi_{\rm raw}=\sum_{a\in\{0,1\}^k}\prod_{C\in P'}|\{v\in C:a_v=1\}|\); multiplicity-weighted raw leaves are not distinct SAT witnesses or mandatory machine work.
The new connected, partially paired, in-target SAT cycle \(F_h\) has \(k=6h,m=8h,q=2h\), unmatched positives \(abc,def\), unmatched negatives \(abd,cef\), and complementary positive/negative OR pairs on \(ab e_{i+1},cdf\). Its disjoint positive supports achieve \(\Psi_{\rm raw}=12^q\) against only \(7^q\) distinct positive-feasible assignments; the explicit source witness \(a=d=1\), \(b=c=e=f=0\) directly decides the recognizable family in polynomial bit work.
Two genuine source-consistent OR branch levels yield three ordinary conditioned children and nine grandchildren, all \emph{outside} \(\mathcal C_\star\). The fixed-header fully serialized grandchild lengths satisfy \(N'=N-O(\log k)=N^{1+o(1)}\), not power shrink. The prior \(3^q\operatorname{poly}(N)\) \emph{actual factor-solving upper algorithm} remains valid because it is not the raw enumerator. A \(\mathcal C_\star\)-only routine needs a separately charged typed adapter before it can receive these children.
Deterministic reproduction: 831 assertions, zero failures, including both labels, abstract overlap controls and a fresh replay of the archived 198-variable monotone UNSAT certificate. Work traffic and emitted-bit-write floors are distinguished from unmeasured full-machine flips, cumulative bit-time, and complete FLMV space. No favorable uniform all-input ordinary-SAT successor or independent worst-case post-acquisition potential follows.

\par\noindent\textbf{CONTEXT-MEET-FIREWALL130 (25 October 2026, LSPV-2): exact hereditary batch-profile obstruction.}

$A\subseteq U$ has batch profile
$\phi_{\mathcal B}(A)_j={\bf1}[A\cap B_j\ne\varnothing]
=\bigvee_{u\in A}{\bf1}[u\in B_j]$.
If $\mathcal R$ is the OR-closure of public membership columns,
a detached fixed-length summary answering this \emph{fixed menu}
for every $A$ requires at least $\lceil\log_2|\mathcal R|\rceil$
bits. New exact \emph{meet-congruence} criterion:
an operation $H$ with
$\phi(A\cap C)=H(\phi(A),\phi(C))$ for
\emph{all} $A,C\subseteq U$ exists if and only if the menu
contains every singleton $\{u\}$ for $u$ in $V=\bigcup_jB_j$.
Necessity is witnessed by $A=U$, $A'=U\setminus\{u\}$,
$C=\{u\}$ when $\{u\}$ is missing; sufficiency follows by
recovering all relevant membership coordinates. In the
meet-complete case, $|\mathcal R|=2^{|V|}$, so no
sub-$|V|$-bit universal detached interface survives arbitrary
future intersections. This refines prior CUTSET-TABLE58
and INTERFACE-ADAPTIVITY73 results; it \emph{does not}

\par\noindent\textbf{IDENTITY-SWEEP129 (25 October 2026, LSPV-2): exact source-name union sweep and low-width approximation bridge.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only on validated source-scoped streams; COMPUTATIONAL finite controls; peer review pending.}
For a valid original-source-named OPEN/CLAUSE/CLOSE stream with cut live sets $S_i$ and $W=\max_i|S_i|$, the number of consistent boundary pin pairs on a cut edge $(i,j)$ is $Q(i,j)=2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$, as established in OVERLAP-CUT128. A new backward event sweep computes each exact $|S_i\cup S_j|$ without materializing any intermediate named live set: reversing CLOSE$(v)$ adds one to the union count and records $v$ as pending, and reversing OPEN$(v)$ subtracts one exactly when $v$ was pending. CLAUSE leaves the count unchanged. Using a $b$-window cyclic shortest-path DP and an $O(b)$-entry pending-name dictionary gives \emph{exact} minimization of $\widehat C(P)=\sum_{(i,j)\in P}2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}$ in $O(Lb\log(b+1))$ deterministic name comparisons and $O(Lb)$ bounded-bit arithmetic, with $O(b)$ value-only numerical/name entries \emph{after separately charged validation}. Dense name stamps give $O(Lb)$ word operations at the cost of $O(V+b)$ working words. Direct predecessor traceback uses additional $O(L)$ entries. Here $L$ is event count, $b$ the maximum block length, and $V$ the number of named variables.

\textbf{New exact global approximation bridge:} If $P_{\times}$ minimizes the older product proxy $C_{\times}(P)=\sum2^{|S_i|+|S_j|}$, and $P_\cup$ minimizes $\widehat C$, then
\[
\widehat C(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\times)\le C_\times(P_\cup)\le2^W\widehat C(P_\cup).
\]
Thus in the promised $W=o(\log N)$ regime, the faster width-bucket planner of CUT126 and streamed traceback of REPLAY127 lose at most an $N^{o(1)}$ multiplicative factor in the \emph{eager compatible-pair enumeration count}, preserving that particular exponent without paying the exact named-sweep planning cost $L^{3/2+o(1)}$ at $b\asymp\sqrt L$. This does not bound \emph{unavoidable} SAT calls, provide an ordinary all-input normalizer, or give a favorable jointly achieved SAT exponent pair.

A complete standard-library extended experiment, seed 202610250112, checked 560 matched SAT/UNSAT budget cases, 900 random scoped traces, 20 persistent-width controls, 55 brute compatible-pair counts, 80 brute SAT/UNSAT labels and 1460 approximation assertions; 233060 candidate edges examined, zero mismatches. A label-blind source-aware control directly decided all 40 SAT and 40 UNSAT examples by checked witness or checked core, bypassing cut enumeration. Python counts are \emph{not} physical random-access Turing-machine bit operations, complete-state flips or peak live-bit measurements. Original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 complete executable remains unrecovered: its Drive continuity log names a transient local ZIP only. \textbf{ARC-EDGE-90 remains OPEN; P versus NP remains OPEN.}

\par\noindent\textbf{OVERLAP-CUT128 (25 October 2026, LSPV-2): exact named-overlap candidate count and global cut optimization.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL finite tests; independent review pending.}
For a \emph{certified valid closed} original-source scoped stream with cut sets $S_i$, the number of compatible assignments to two neighboring cuts is
\[
 Q(i,j)=2^{|S_i\cup S_j|}=2^{|S_i|+|S_j|-|S_i\cap S_j|},
\]
rather than the prior product upper proxy $2^{|S_i|+|S_j|}$. This follows from a bijection between compatible pairs and assignments to the union. The complete eager-enumeration candidate objective $\widehat C(P)=\sum_{(i,j)\in P}Q(i,j)$ has an exact last-edge dynamic program in $O(Lb(W+1))$ word operations using $O(L(W+1))$ stored numerical/name-list entries, after a charged named-stream validity pass; $W$ is maximum live width and $b$ maximum block length. Costs are $O(W+\log L)$ bits each. This is an \emph{upper-cost model of one selected enumeration}, not an arbitrary-decider lower bound. CUT126 and REPLAY127 remain correct minimizers of the older product proxy, but their width-only bucket technique is not automatically exact for this identity-sensitive objective.
On connected named sources with $h$ persistent original variables, $10h$ proper ternary clauses and $L=12h$ valid events, matched SAT/UNSAT labels with identical unsigned supports, $b=4h$, both optimal schedules use cuts $(0,4h,8h,12h)$ but the old objective is $2^{2h}+2^{h+1}$ versus the true candidate count $3\cdot2^h$. The ratio $(2^h+2)/3$ grows exponentially in $h$, \emph{outside the sublog-width ARC contractor promise}. Finite checks: 1525 assertion groups, zero failures, 450 variable-lifetime traces, 116 differing best cut schedules; on a matched 45-event both-label control, the old optimum costs 2056 under the new objective against new optimum 776. These are abstract counts, not physical bit-machine traces; discovery, validation, child headers, pin construction, strategy switching, caching, reconstructing, exact SAT calls, peak live bits, state flips and bit-time remain independently charged. \par\noindent\textbf{CYCLE-AFFINE-SWITCH123: chord-source joins versus Gaussian bypass
(PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL tests; ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
For a \emph{validated} multigraph of $B$ local two-CNF-plus-one-parity blocks, each shared Boolean variable belonging to exactly two blocks and representing one labelled edge, let $q=|E|-B+c$ for $c$ connected components. A charged spanning forest and exhaustive assignments to the $q$ off-tree source variables reduce the remaining joins to TREE-AFFINE-JOIN122. Exact word upper bounds are $O(2^qL^2)$ time and $O(L+q)$ working words; indexed bit-machine simulation has ordinary logarithmic overhead. This is classical cycle-cutset conditioning in a source-matched specialization, not a novel FPT dichotomy.

The connected cubic prism $P_r$ with $2r$ vertices and $3r$ edges gives ordinary proper three-CNF parity formulas $F_r^{+}$ and $F_r^{-}$ of $n=3r$ variables, $m=8r$ clauses and identical unsigned supports. The two parity-charge choices yield SAT and UNSAT, respectively. Their edge-labelled cycle rank is $q=r+1$ and affine row rank $2r-1$; deleting any complete vertex parity gadget from the UNSAT source restores SAT, hence any UNSAT clause core has at least $2r$ clauses. Yet a charged canonical recognizer plus parity scan decides both labels in $O(N\operatorname{polylog}N)$ elementary bit work and $O(\log N)$ writable bits; arbitrary validated XOR3 conjunctions are decidable by polynomial Gaussian elimination. The edge variables have $4$ positive and $4$ negative occurrences, \emph{not} the strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ quotas. Thus neither cycle rank nor core span forces arbitrary-machine SAT continuation work. A declared exhaustive chord policy checked $2^{13}=8192$ assignments and 1,572,864 local table rows at $r=12$ UNSAT, while Gaussian used 23 row XORs; these are different uncalibrated logical costs, not comparable FLMV bit metrics. The extended audit completed 335 assertion groups with zero failures across 14 prism sources, 160 small general CNF controls and five binary/Horn controls. Its finite state-adaptive policy chose SCC, Horn, affine or exact fallback without a label oracle; switching costs were counted logically. \par\noindent\textbf{SELECTOR-TRIM121: exact nonempty witness selector and
compiler-size correction (PROVED/EXACT within declared representation;
ARC-EDGE-90 and P versus NP remain OPEN).}
For each original proper ternary clause, introduce three selectors and
require their OR, not parity-one or exactly-one.  One implication per
occurrence to the original source literal gives the exact existential
source relation; alternatively conflict clauses forbid selecting
complementary occurrences of any common original variable, permitting
source-consistent reconstruction.  No intra-clause triangle constraints
are necessary.  For strict signed exact-$(2,2)$, $4n=3m$ and
$3m$ complement edges form disjoint $K_{2,2}$ components.  The mixed
formula has $m$ positive ternary selectors and $3m$ negative binary
conflicts on $3m$ selectors.  Binary-to-proper-ternary replacement using
a fresh private auxiliary per edge yields exactly $6m$ target variables
and $7m$ proper ternary clauses.  The older parity-plus-triangle compiler
correctly used $9m$ variables and $16m$ clauses but was not size-optimal.
For the canonical $198/264$ pair, the new ordinary output has $1584$
variables, $1848$ clauses, $133092$ fixed-record bits, against
$321062$ bits for the old output, a $58.54632\%$ reduction, but still
larger than the $15870$-bit original source.  Therefore the inherited
ordinary-child scale is $\lambda=1$, not $1/2$, and there is no
all-input favorable ARC edge.  The named-source implication 2CNF
has a directed acyclic implication graph of path depth at most two;
the source-free negative-conflict 2CNF has depth one.  Both are
individually SAT, while their globally consistent conjunction with
positive selector OR blocks remains NP-complete on the strict input
language.  This does not prove unconditional decision-time hardness.
The fully charged finite source/hybrid audit tested $556$ formulas
($514$ SAT, $42$ UNSAT), with $170$ matching certificate routes,
$383$ exact fallback routes and zero label discrepancies.  Peak
machine bits, actual flips and cumulative bit-time were not measured.

\par\noindent\textbf{SCC-ODD-ORBIT120: exact rank-one parity switch over 2-CNF
(PROVED/EXACT restricted algorithm, COMPUTATIONAL independent controls;
ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
Let $B$ be a source-valid explicit 2-CNF on $n$ variables and $m$
clauses, and let $\bigoplus_{i\in S}x_i=d$ be one global parity row.
Compute implication SCCs and pair each component with its literal
complement. Each pair $P$ has analytical parity weight
$W_P=|\{i\in S:P(i)=P\}|\bmod2$. Any two models of $B$ are connected
by a path of satisfying assignments that flips one paired SCC group
at a time in reverse condensation topological order. Given one $B$
model, a source-paid opposite-unit 2-SAT test for each odd-weight
group decides whether target parity is reachable; on a flexible
group, the same path constructs a verified witness. This exact,
uniform \emph{restricted mixed-language} algorithm uses
$O(n(n+m+1))$ word time and $O(n+m+1)$ peak working words in one
realization. Its bit-cost implementation charges parsing, SCC calls,
failed unit tests, switches and source replay; value flips and
cumulative live bit-time are separate unmeasured coordinates.
The \emph{classification} of every fixed affine-rank rung is already
known from Brakensiek--Gopi--Guruswami's 2-SAT-MOD theorem, recorded
by LSPV-5 FIXED-SYNDROME-FIREWALL113. This run supplies the explicit
SCC-orbit constructive proof, not a novel fixed-rank dichotomy.
The verified compact audit independently classifies $2100$ small
both-label cases and $16$ growing controls with zero mismatches;
a separately instrumented extended audit covers $3520$ small cases.
SELECTOR-SPLIT119 requires $m$ \emph{independent} parity rows, so
repeating this rank-one dispatcher does not furnish an all-input
ordinary-SAT contractor or solve the source-consistent join.

\par\noindent\textbf{SELECTOR-SPLIT119: source-consistent affine/2-CNF compilation firewall
(PROVED/EXACT for the declared compiler; joint all-input ARC resource improvement OPEN).}
For every proper 3-CNF $F$ with $m$ clauses and $n$ source
variables, create a selector $y_{ij}$ for each of the $3m$
literal occurrences. The affine part
$\bigwedge_i(y_{i1}\oplus y_{i2}\oplus y_{i3}=1)$
has $m$ independent equations; the bijunctive part uses
three at-most-one clauses per triple plus one source implication
$y_{ij}\Rightarrow\ell_{ij}$ per occurrence, for exactly $6m$
binary clauses on $3m+n$ Boolean variables. The exact source-coherent
equivalence is
\[
  F\in\mathrm{SAT}\iff A_F\wedge B_F\in\mathrm{SAT}.
\]
Both $A_F$ and $B_F$ are \emph{individually satisfiable for every
input $F$}. To obtain actual \emph{ordinary SAT} children,
compile each XOR3 parity row into four proper ternary clauses
and each binary $(a\vee b)$ into the two proper ternaries
$(a\vee b\vee z)\wedge(a\vee b\vee\neg z)$ with private $z$.
The full ordinary 3-CNF has $16m$ clauses and $9m+n$ variables,
and partitions into $10m$ independently satisfiable
$O(\log(n+m))$-bit named child groups (or $16m$ singleton clauses).
Therefore child SAT labels alone do not decide the parent. This
is a firewall on label-only splitting, \emph{not} on arbitrary
source-aware algorithms or existential ARC closure.
For signed exact-$(2,2)$, the original-source variables can be
eliminated from the bijunctive part, leaving a $K_{2,2}$ per
source variable, $4n=3m$ opposite-sign edges, $3m$ clause
triangle edges, and $6m$ binary clauses on $3m$ selectors,
each of degree four. This is equivalent to the known
independent-transversal formulation, not a new NP-completeness theorem.
Under the declared bit-record encoding, the canonical $198/264$
benchmark has input $N=15{,}870$ bits but materialized children
totaling $103{,}558$ output bits in the \emph{mixed-language} form.\nThe fully ordinary proper-3CNF source contains $4{,}224$ clauses,\n$2{,}574$ variables and $321{,}062$ written bits. Mandatory one-pass input reads
plus writes cost at least $119{,}428$ bit operations before control;
eager sequential storage needs at least $85{,}572$ writable child bits.
A fully lazy alternative must charge child bit generation on demand.
Actual state flips and bit-time were not measured.
Executed tests: 162 small original-source/selector comparisons,
136 SAT and 26 UNSAT, 5350 checks with zero mismatches;
the named hybrid uses a matching certificate on 47 inputs
and an exact exponential fallback on 115. The $198/264$ SAT
neighbor's witness was replayed; UNSAT status for its paired source
was \emph{imported} from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 and not newly proved.
The user-facing complete execution archive and compact GitHub
reproducer differ in coverage and are labeled accordingly.

\par\noindent\textbf{FALLBACK-PADDING118: charged black-box parent-fallback firewall
(PROVED/EXACT within declared conditional compiler model; ARC-EDGE-90 OPEN).}
Let $\mathcal C$ be polynomial-time recognizable with infinitely many
growing ordinary SAT sources outside it.  Consider only a black-box
hybrid $H[A]$ which uses certified polynomial specialists on
$\mathcal C$ and otherwise calls the supplied guest $A$ on the
\emph{unchanged full $N$-bit parent}, awaiting its answer.
Under the counterfactual $P=NP$, choose any fixed uniform exact
polynomial SAT decider $D$ with time exponent $d$ and space exponent
$s$. For each sufficiently large fixed integer $K>d$, form the
total uniform exact guest $A_K$ by running at least $N^K$ charged
counter steps in $O(K\log N)$ mutable bits and then executing $D$.
The single machine $A_K$ has simultaneous exponents
$(K,\max\{s,0\})$ with $o(1)$ slack, yet
\[
 \max_{|F|=N}T_{H[A_K]}(F)\ge N^K
\]
along an unbounded sequence of lengths outside $\mathcal C$.
Thus this \emph{specified} black-box recognizer with an unmodified
parent-scale fallback cannot uniformly guarantee $K-\varepsilon$
time-exponent contraction over all supplied guests.
The result does \emph{not} refute existential closure of the achievable
region $E$, because under $P=NP$ the alternative faster machine $D$
exists; it also does not exclude code-aware or other adaptive
all-input algorithms, fractional source-consistent children or
independent machine-existence theorems.

The executed finite adaptive controls acquire source parameters, try
2-SAT SCC, Horn, dual-Horn and grouped-XOR Gaussian elimination,
and charge failed recognition and full-parent exact DPLL fallback.
Of 54 cases (45 SAT, nine UNSAT), all independent truth-table
labels agree: policy routes 2/5/5/2/40 respectively, with zero
failures. Relative to a specified padded baseline, the hybrid
avoids 1,323,244 of 6,349,564 billed dummy bit-events on these
inputs. These Python events are not a physical FLMV
time/space simulation, and the growing connected UNSAT controls
retain a fixed contradictory core. Separate accumulator value-flips
are not necessary work: unchanged writes remain charged.
The prior CHILD-SCALE-PREFIX117 hereditary-width denominator
counterexample also remains valid:
$w=o(\log N)$ and $L\le N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ with
$w_{\rm child}\le w+O(1)$ do not imply
$w_{\rm child}=o(\log L)$ without a \emph{lower} descendant
length bound or explicit child-promise validation.
The complete HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 generator is still unrecovered.
The retired cosine product endpoint is not used.

\par\noindent\textbf{TORUS-EXACT22-FRONTIER116, strict signed source-frontier firewall
(PROVED/EXACT original-clause model; general ARC closure OPEN).}
A new family improves the earlier unbounded-occurrence complete-graph
frontier control to \emph{proper, connected, signed exact-$(2,2)$}
3-CNFs. For each multiple of three $h\ge6$ there are opposite-label
sources with identical unsigned supports, two sign changes,
\[
 n=3h^2+198,\quad m=4h^2+264,\quad
 N=(4m+2)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil.
\]
Every source has exactly two positive and two negative occurrences.
For \emph{any} ordering retaining the original toroidal grid clauses
and shared source identifiers, at the balanced grid-edge cut
\[
 w\ge\lceil h/2\rceil
 =\Omega(\sqrt{N/\log N}).
\]
Proof: fewer than $h/2$ mixed vertices leave more than $h/2$
intact rows and columns; all their $2h(h-b)>h^2$ edges have the
same color, contradicting the balanced bipartition of $2h^2$ edges.
Both labels remain easy under canonical source recognition:
$O(N\,\operatorname{polylog}N)$ time and $O(\log N)$
work bits on the \emph{recognized} family, with exact exponential
fallback on unsupported inputs. Neither Hall clause matching
($m>n$) nor complementary-pivot contraction can initially terminate.
The original fixed 254-clause UNSAT core persists, so this is
\emph{not} hardness amplification and does not forbid source-aware
rewrites or another all-input contraction.
Six sizes through $h=192$ ($N=14{,}181{,}168$ bits, width bound $96$)
and 164 independently brute-forced small controls
(126 SAT, 38 UNSAT) passed; the source-local 537-node UNSAT
certificate and 252-clause six-module coverage were independently
rechecked. P versus NP remains OPEN.

\par\noindent\textbf{FLMV-CURVE-FRONTIER115, contraction source-tranche (PROVED/EXACT only in declared models; all-input SAT closure OPEN).}
Re-examining the original FLMV Theorem~3.1 and Corollaries~3.2/5.8
repairs MULTICHANNEL1's old endpoint: the purported universal
$t+s<2\cos(\pi/7)$ SAT landing is \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}.
The correct forbidden curve is
\[
\beta(c)=\tfrac12\left((c+2)/c^2-c\right),
\qquad \beta(241/200)=\frac{11642479}{23232400}>\tfrac12.
\]
For a genuinely all-input, fully charged, self-contained recursive
SAT contractor with finite channels
$(\eta_j,\theta_j)$, $\theta_j<1$, local work exponent $r$,
and local/child peak exponent $\rho$, the exact
conditional closure bound is
\[
C=\max\{r,\max_j\eta_j/(1-\theta_j)\},\qquad
\rho=\max\{\sigma,\max_j\theta_j\}.
\]
If $C>1$ and $\rho<\beta(C)$, the asserted contractor contradicts
the known FLMV endpoint; therefore such a universal contractor
cannot satisfy all those premises.
This is a strict \emph{firewall}, not a construction of the
missing all-input machine or of existential closure of the jointly
achievable region $E$.

An independent connected proper 3CNF both-label family, consisting
of every edge clause $(x_i\vee x_j\vee z_{ij})$ on $K_h$ and one
constant-size contradictory/satisfiable control gadget, has
$N=\Theta(h^2\log h)$ and, in \emph{every} unchanged-clause scoped
ordering, live frontier
\[
w\ge\min\{u:\tbinom u2\ge\lfloor\tbinom h2/2\rfloor\}
=\Omega\!\left(\sqrt{N/\log N}\right).
\]
At $h=256$ the certified lower bound is $182$, whereas
$\log_2N\simeq21.518$. Yet a uniform charged source recognizer
directly decides both labels in polynomial time; its general exact
fallback is exponential. This refutes clause \emph{reordering}
as universal sublog-width normalization and raw width as a time
lower bound, without excluding symbolic preprocessing or any other
all-input ARC successor. Extended audits passed 974 frontier
checks and 83 exact-rational/finite-route checks, no failures.
The unproved link remains cofinal finite charged entry into a
model-matched forbidden box, with one uniform SAT machine and
simultaneous resources at each node. Independent review pending.

\par\noindent\textbf{Earlier PROOF-SUPPORT73: paid single-pass UNSAT proof provenance, and a strict exact-$(2,2)$ prime-interface firewall (PROVED/EXACT restricted, COMPUTATIONAL scaling; independent review pending).}
For a source-certified local component $H_j(Y_j,U_j)$, a checked
source-labelled DPLL tree under assumptions $\alpha$ can annotate unit
implications and source conflicts by the assumptions and branch choices
on which they depend. Eliminating each split token only after both child
refutations yields a sufficient original assumption subset
$\beta\subseteq\alpha$, and the sound learned no-good
$D_\beta=\bigvee_{(v=b)\in\beta}(v\ne b)$.
One dependency pass plus one independent replay suffices, polynomial in
the \emph{explicit certificate size}, not necessarily in original input
size. Original proof acquisition, source reads, annotation, switching,
failed attempts, full witness replay and peak live space remain charged.
This is the known proof-core principle in a newly measured adaptive
policy, not a general SAT shortcut.

On a connected proper (but \emph{not} signed exact-$(2,2)$)
both-label family of guarded contradictory XOR chains with $r=6$ and
$k$ irrelevant boundary sources, the specified local UNSAT DPLL proof
has 127 nodes. At $k=4,16,32,64$, repeated proof-replay minimization
uses $75{,}860$, $385{,}772$, $995{,}212$, $2{,}886{,}860$
source-clause scans, including a rejected deletion attempt; the
provenance pass and one checker use respectively
$37{,}608$, $48{,}120$, $62{,}136$, $90{,}168$ scans and learn the
same unit. This only compares named proof-processing policies: a direct
affine recognizer may avoid the XOR refutation entirely.

On the original strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ $198/264$ benchmark,
each of the six learned local no-goods is prime over its actual source
interface: all 15 single-assumption deletions have replayed SAT local
witnesses, and provenance extracts no smaller support. A uniform
128-node direct probe solves the SAT neighbor at node 104; the original
UNSAT input needs the prior exact boundary continuation.
The tested adaptive policy uses 18 local calls/725 local nodes, worse
than LAZY-BOUNDARY71's 16/688, so no dominance claim is made.
Three independently run experiment modes pass 1,432 recorded assertion
groups with zero failures, including 640 independent small source
restrictions, 12 renaming/polarity controls, and a damaged-certificate
rejection. All-decider representation/decision coverage and any
superpolynomial charged potential remain \OPEN{}; $P=NP$ and
$P\ne NP$ both remain unresolved.

\par\noindent\textbf{Prior CORE-REPLAY72: checked proof-core weakening and state-adaptive exact master. PROVED/EXACT restricted; COMPUTATIONAL controls; P versus NP OPEN.}
For a source-verified decomposition
\[
H=G(Y)\land\bigwedge_j H_j(Y_j,U_j)\subseteq F
\]
with disjoint private sources, a checked DPLL refutation of
$H_j$ under assumptions $\alpha$ can be replayed under
$\beta\subseteq\alpha$. If the independent checker accepts the
\emph{same} proof under $\beta$, the stronger no-good
$D_\beta=\bigvee_{(v=b)\in\beta}(v\ne b)$
is a source-entailment. At most $|Y_j|$ attempted assumption deletions
are made, with all failed proof replays and source access charged.
The exact master solves $G\wedge\bigwedge_j D_{\beta_j}$, rather than
enumerating all boundary rows. An UNSAT master implies parent UNSAT
through source-checked subformula inclusion; SAT requires reconstruction
and full original-source clause replay. A uniform total policy uses a
bounded initial direct DPLL probe and an exact original-source fallback.
Proof size and boundary support are acquired state parameters, not
labels or runtime-defined invariants. A 16-node observed proof cutoff
skips costly attempts.

On a new \emph{connected, proper three-literal} opposite-label family
(not signed exact-$(2,2)$) with $n=2k+5,m=2k+8$, the earlier
full-row policy uses exactly $2^k$ local UNSAT calls whereas proof
replay learns one unit clause from one local call, with two exact
master calls and $O(k^2)$ charged word work; the supplied dense bit-mask solver stores $O(k^2)$ mask bits plus $O(k)$ source records, while a sparse-clause realization may use $O(k)$ working words.
Checked old-call values were $2,8,32,128,512$ at
$k=1,3,5,7,9$, compared with $1$ new local call each; the
new policy also ran with one call at $k=16,32$.
All SAT controls used one new call. A randomized source-coherent
audit covered 140 six-variable instances (51 SAT, 89 UNSAT);
125 of 253 attempted assumption deletions succeeded, with
independently replayed source proofs.

On the connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ benchmark U198/264,
all six local refutations exceeded the cutoff; no assumptions
could be deleted by the tested rule. It used 18 local calls and
725 local nodes, \emph{worse} than LAZY-BOUNDARY71's 16 calls and
688 nodes. The matching SAT neighbor V is instead solved by the
uniform initial 128-node probe at node 104. This is
\emph{not} a universal improvement or an unrestricted time lower
bound. General proof memory, solver time, separator acquisition,
and the all-decider representation-versus-computation bridge remain
\OPEN{}. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN.

\par\noindent\textbf{Prior LAZY-BOUNDARY71: charged state-adaptive source-interface no-good learning, PROVED/EXACT restricted, independently checked finite evidence, external review pending.}
For a source-verified clause subformula
\[
H=G(Y)\land\bigwedge_{j=1}^{J}H_j(Y_j,U_j)\subseteq F,
\qquad U_i\cap U_j=\varnothing\ \ (i\ne j),\quad Y_j\subseteq Y,
\]
an exact local UNSAT decision under $\alpha|_{Y_j}$ proves the no-good
$D_{j,\alpha}=\bigvee_{y\in Y_j}(y\ne\alpha(y))$.
It may be learned without materializing every positive interface row.
All learned clauses are source-entailments; an UNSAT master proves the original
parent UNSAT even if the verified subformula omitted cross-clauses.
A positive abstract witness must be reconstructed and replayed against
\emph{all} of $F$ before SAT is accepted.
With source-closed positive-result caching and permanent negative blocking,
at most $\sum_j2^{|Y_j|}$ distinct local solver calls occur in this
declared algorithm, although the master may still perform $2^{|Y|}$
candidate tests per refinement and local solves can be expensive.
This is a constructive source-coherent call bound, not an arbitrary-decider
time lower bound.

On the previously source-certified connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$
$198$-variable/$264$-clause UNSAT $U$ and its two-sign SAT neighbor $V$,
both initial Hall and tautological-pivot shortcuts fail. The previous
eager six-module projection used $36$ local calls/$1038$ nodes/$86856$
clause scans on $U$. The new lazy positive-cache policy used
$16$ calls/$688$ nodes/$62878$ scans; no cache required
$21$ calls/$764$ nodes/$67708$ scans. Six checked negative
no-good clauses refute the six-variable master, with $537$
independently checked UNSAT proof nodes and \emph{no}
enumeration of the $30$ positive boundary rows.
A total exact policy first probes the entire original input for $128$
nodes: $V$ produces a replayed SAT witness at node $104$,
whereas $U$ exhausts the probe, switches to the lazy interface,
and closes in $128+688+3=819$ charged search nodes, plus source
parsing, candidate tests, cache operation costs, proof output
and checking. A support-first order makes only $15$ calls
but \emph{more} clause scans ($63190$ versus $62878$).
Thus even within a finite hybrid menu there is no scalar best
choice without declaring the resource. Generic complete-separator
tests gave $58$ SAT and $82$ UNSAT over $140$ independently brute-forced
small formulas; $145$ negative local proof trees were checked.
All $335$ recorded assertions passed. The full benchmark has ten
cross-module clauses omitted only from its UNSAT-preserving subformula;
the abstract SAT branch is forbidden from accepting without full replay.

\par\noindent\textbf{Latest HALL-SWITCH70: source-coherent Hall witness after paid pair contraction (PROVED/EXACT in a restricted certificate model; external review pending).}
In strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper unique 3CNF, $4n=3m$, so the original clauses cannot be injectively matched to $n$ sources. After certified complementary-pair contraction, $p$ pairs replace $2p$ ternaries by $p$ binaries and leave $m'=m-p$ reduced clauses pointwise equivalent to the original source. A bipartite matching saturating all $m'$ reduced clause nodes with \emph{distinct original source variables} yields an exact SAT witness by satisfying the corresponding matched literal in each clause; all original clauses are then replayed. Sorting, pair recognition, actual failed Hopcroft--Karp searches, switching and witness verification take
\[
O(m\log(m+2)+(n+m)\sqrt{n+m})
\]
addressed-word time and $O(n+m)$ peak words for the certificate step. Hall failure is not an UNSAT determination. The theorem applies to a recognized matching certificate, not to every possible SAT decider.

The new connected shifted-permutation strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ family has $n=6q,m=8q,p=2q,\delta=4q$. For fixed-point-free socket permutations, all $2q$ eligible residual pivots fail the earlier CANCEL-JOIN69 \emph{tautological} test, yet a $6q$-clause saturating Hall matching yields an immediate SAT witness with no selector enumeration. On unshifted modules, the adaptive policy instead cancels the $4q$ ternaries and switches to SCC. The q=256 shifted audit checked $1{,}536$ variables, $2{,}048$ original clauses, $1{,}024$ unmatched ternaries, 0 taut pivots, 1,536 source-distinct clause matches, 27,670 actual matching edge scans and original-source witness replay. An additional true structural counterexample combines either member of the earlier $198$-variable opposite-label pair with 66 disjoint PAIRPATH73 gadgets: it has $m'=n=594$ but 264 core clauses see only 198 source variables, so it fails Hall on both labels. The UNSAT label of the D\"ocker core is \emph{imported} from DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 rather than re-proved in this run. Overall finite evidence: 720 assertion-accounted checks, 0 failures, including separate small SAT/UNSAT fallback controls; independent peer review pending.

\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a required exponential continuation merely because $\delta$ is large, the initial tautological-pivot test fails, or a complete backward selector service has exponentially many candidates. \textbf{OPEN:} the proposed all-decider charged parameterized potential, structural-growth and representation/decision bridging theorems. All adaptive source-aware algorithms remain legal; P versus NP remains \OPEN{}. Verified standalone report, reproducible executable, raw summary and handoff:

\par\noindent\textbf{CANCEL-JOIN69: Charged state-adaptive source-coherent pivot cancellation in strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ proper 3CNF (PROVED/EXACT restricted; independent peer review pending).}
After unique complementary-pivot contraction $F\equiv B\land T$,
original signed-source quotas impose
\[
t_x+2b_x^++u_x^+=2,\qquad t_x+2b_x^-+u_x^-=2.
\]
If source $x$ pivots exactly once, then $b_x^\pm=0$ and $u_x^\pm=1$,
so the two remaining ternaries $(x\vee A),(\neg x\vee D)$ admit the
\emph{known} Davis--Putnam existential projection
$\exists x[(x\vee A)(\neg x\vee D)]\equiv(A\vee D)$.
When the resolvent contains a complementary pair, it is tautological:
both source clauses can be deleted and $x$ reconstructed from an explicit
mate, with full original-source witness replay. This is a new \emph{charged
application} in the current strict signed-source calculus, not a newly
invented general resolution rule. Unlike SCC-ISOLATION68's idle binary-SCC
prepass, it acts directly on unmatched ternaries.

A cyclic connected strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ family $C_q$ has
$n=6q,m=8q$, exactly $2q$ complementary pairs, initial unmatched
defect $\delta=4q$ and minimum \emph{postpair} strong 2CNF deletion
backdoor $h=q$. Exactly $2q$ local tautological pivot eliminations remove
all $4q$ unmatched clauses, leaving $2q$ binary clauses; choosing
all $b_i=c_i=1$ and inverse-tracing $x_i=y_i=\neg z_i$ provides a
checkable satisfying original-source witness. Parsing, forced pairing,
failed/successful mate checks, live incidence updates, mode switching,
SCC and complete replay take $O(m\log(m+2)+n)$ addressed-word time and
$O(n+m)$ peak live words on this recognized family. A \emph{restricted}
nonpruning fixed-backdoor enumerator has $2^q$ leaves, but this gives
no arbitrary-algorithm lower bound. A $q=2$ two-negative-socket hostile
swap remains connected, unique-clause, and balanced, while only two of
four pivot eliminations remain tautological, requiring bounded non-taut
resolution plus exact DPLL. The source quota formula must be recomputed
from actual live incidences after the first projection.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 13,287 assertion-accounted checks and zero
failures, with q values through $512$ ($n=3072,m=4096$,
$N=311334$ encoded bits, $\delta=2048$ eliminated). Original/projection
exhaustive checks were restricted to $n\le12$. The separately certified
D\"ocker opposite-label $198$-variable/$264$-clause pair is not simplified
by this prepass: $p=0,\delta=264$ on both labels. Numerical HiGHS reports
infeasible/feasible respectively, but no new independently checkable UNSAT
proof was produced. Disjoint additions of the easy cyclic family preserve
the hard residual while eliminating the easy component. Generic
unsupported inputs retain total exact DPLL fallback with no polynomial
claim. \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} a mandatory every-input exponential
runtime lower bound derived only from initial unmatched defect,
strong backdoor size, selected occurrence count or optional rich service.

The three universal obligations remain \OPEN{}: a noncircular
decision-only progress bound against \emph{every} uniform deterministic exact
SAT decider, its independently justified growing parameter on suitable
algorithm-dependent inputs, and fully charged all-method coverage.
Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ has been proved. Verified source:

\par\noindent\textbf{SCC-ISOLATION68: PROVED/EXACT in certified strict signed exact-$(2,2)$ pivot-contraction calculus; independent review pending.}
Complementary-pivot pairs contract source-exactly to binary clauses $B$,
leaving unmatched ternaries $T$. Original occurrence conservation is
\[
 t_x+2b_x^++u_x^+=2,\qquad t_x+2b_x^-+u_x^-=2.
\]
Every source literal belonging to a nontrivial implication SCC of $B$
must occur with both signs in $B$, hence its source variable has
$u_x^+=u_x^-=0$ and is absent from $T$. Therefore a paid SCC-equivalence
prepass over \emph{only these} binary pivot contractions can never shorten
the remaining ternaries. This is a source-grammar-specific
\textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED transfer} of the QUIET-EQUIV67 shortcut;
QUIET-EQUIV67 remains a valid polynomial mixed-width result under its
different hypotheses. No SCC operation after a partial source assignment
is ruled out. The binary graph has degree at most two; PAIRPATH73's
existing exact existential path/cycle projection yields a matching of
binary clauses over \emph{distinct ternary boundary source pairs},
which cannot be discarded merely because $B$ forces no individual literal.
An additional source-count corollary rules out renamable Horn and
renamable dual-Horn forms \emph{before} source transformation because
each sign has $2n>m=4n/3$ occurrences.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 265 assertion groups, zero failures. Strict
paired-cycle, affine-quartet and six-variable connected projected-path
controls were checked; the paid adaptive policy selected path projection,
source-coherent branch/SCC, affine reconstruction or an exact fallback
on equal-size 240-variable, 320-clause SAT/UNSAT controls containing the
authenticated 198-variable D\"ocker opposite-label pair. The independent
HiGHS experiment returned SAT and UNSAT respectively, but did not produce
a new formal UNSAT refutation; previous DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 remains the
certificate anchor. Skipping the idle pre-SCC pass saves only a redundant
linear implication scan, not a new worst-case exponent. The complete
all-decider rich-service/expenditure implication and the conditional
$T_A(F)\ge2^{a r_A(F)}/N^b$ target remain \OPEN{}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (QUIET-EQUIV67, peer review pending).}
A paid SCC-equivalence quotient can collapse genuine ternary clauses
\emph{without any initial unit or binary-backbone forced source literal}.
For $q\ge2$, the connected mixed-width opposite-label family
$F_q^\pm$ has $n=3q$, $m=7q-2$, $q$ variable-disjoint ternaries,
and raw syntactic strong 2-CNF backdoor $h=q$.
The binary backbone equates all $3q$ source variables yet has precisely
two satisfying assignments and no forced literal.
All-positive ternaries give SAT; changing only the last ternary to
all-negative gives UNSAT with identical unsigned supports and encoded length.
One charged SCC pass on the binary backbone merges every positive source
literal into one class and every negative source literal into another;
each ternary becomes a unit. A second SCC decides both labels with
$25q-8$ implication arcs total, $O(N/w)=O(q)$ addressed-word work,
$O(q)$ live logical words, and zero search branches, including
recognition and original-source SAT witness replay.
By contrast a \emph{specifically defined} complete strong-backdoor
enumerator uses exactly $2^q$ child calls on either label.
This comparison constrains that policy alone, not arbitrary DPLL or
other uniform exact SAT algorithms.
The mixed-width family is \emph{not} proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF.
The strict 198-variable, 264-clause opposite-label D\"ocker source
pair and saturated affine-quartet controls are not solved by this
binary-SCC recognizer; an exact fallback is retained. The original
UNSAT label relies on the earlier DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 rather than
a newly regenerated proof.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 32 assertion-accounted case groups, zero failures;
scalable matched SAT/UNSAT inputs through $q=512$ and strict-source
non-transfer controls. This falsifies an initial raw backdoor size
as a universal exponential runtime potential, but it does \emph{not}
prove a decision-only trajectory potential or any $P$ versus $NP$
separation. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain \OPEN{}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (ADAPTIVE-BIDIR65, independent review pending).}
Forward and reverse SAT reasoning can change source-coherent representations
dynamically. A uniform source-checked policy first extracts forced assignments
from a binary implication subformula, then recognizes independently shared-source
components, complete parity quartets, graph-incidence parity equations,
Horn and 2-SAT. On the explicit mixed both-label family
\[
n=3q+6,\qquad m=8q+7,\qquad k=8q+1,\qquad
N=(32q+30)\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil,
\]
the paired formulas differ in exactly one parity charge and have identical
length and unsigned supports; all-even is SAT and odd total charge is UNSAT.
Each parity source occurs in two equation supports, permitting a linear-time
spanning-forest assignment/contradiction check. The whole adaptive policy
uses $O(N)$ addressed-word time, $O(N)$ live logical words and zero branches
on this recognized family, including recognition and source replay.
The instances are \emph{not} strict signed exact-$(2,2)$: each parity-edge
source has four positive and four negative CNF occurrences.
The SAT trajectory switches binary backbone $\to$ components $\to$
graph parity $\to$ Horn $\to$ SCC; UNSAT ends at graph parity.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 90 both-label policy runs, 180 independently
brute-forced random formulas ($91$ SAT, $89$ UNSAT) tested against five
policies, 27 hand controls, and small $q=4,5$ source-edge exhaustive checks
with no failed assertions. At $q=32$ hybrid UNSAT uses $2{,}332$
instrumented source restriction literal reads and no branches, while a
particular fixed DPLL comparator stops UNKNOWN after 250 branches.
This is not a worst-case lower bound on DPLL, a globally optimal policy,
or a polynomial decider for an NP-complete language.
\textbf{OPEN:} covering every uniform exact decider with a charged
decision-only invariant despite source rereads, direct decision, adaptive
switching, caching and recomputation. Both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$ remain OPEN.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (REPAIR-GAP64; independent review pending).}
For proper signed exact-$(2,2)$ 3CNF, an explicit integer polarity-selector
model has optimum $\mu(F)$, the minimal number of original source variables
selected in both signs by a capacity selector.  Its natural linear relaxation
always has the \emph{same zero optimum} on SAT and UNSAT sources: assign each
occurrence selector $1/3$, each sign-usage indicator $1/2$ and every
mixed-source penalty $0$.  Thus the relaxed model is label-blind.
Furthermore $\mu(F\sqcup G)=\mu(F)+\mu(G)$ for source-disjoint unions; by
D\"ocker's strict-monotone NP-completeness, for \emph{every fixed} $B\ge0$
testing $\mu(F)\le B$ remains NP-complete (reduce $H$ to $B+1$ renamed copies).
These are exact statements about an optional integer program and its LP,
not about work necessary for every SAT machine.

The certified opposite-label 198/264 pair $U,V$ satisfies
$\mu(U)=1$, $\mu(V)=0$, relying on the separately checked
DOECKER-CORE-CERT61 UNSAT certificate and the supplied source-replayed
two-sign-swap SAT witness.  The growing pairs $U^{\sqcup p},V^{\sqcup p}$
have $\mu=p$ versus $0$ and identical fractional objective $0$,
yet a paid canonical-template recognizer decides the entire
recognized block-major family in $O(n+m)$ addressed word operations.
The template family is disconnected; the recognizer is not a general SAT
solver and unsupported inputs need an exact fallback.  The local
stdlib audit checked fifteen matched cases, 36 exhaustive small valid cases
and 9,170 literal-selection trials, general 3CNF UNSAT and 2-SAT/SCC controls,
with zero failed assertions.  The source UNSAT certificate was imported rather
than rerun in this audit.
\textbf{OPEN:} a uniformly constructible all-input polynomial SAT algorithm,
or an all-total-uniform-exact-decider superpolynomial lower bound robust to
source scans, alternative representations, and direct decision.  Neither
$P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$ is proved.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (POLARITY-REPAIR63, independent review pending).}
For signed proper exact-$(2,2)$ source 3CNF with $4n=3m$, a clause-to-two-source-clones bipartite Hall matching always saturates all $m$ original clauses; it deliberately ignores selected polarity and therefore succeeds on source-certified UNSAT inputs as well. Let $\mu(F)$ be the minimum number of source variables selected with both polarities in a capacity-two one-occurrence-per-clause selector. If $d_{\rm SAT}(F)$ is minimum swaps of a positive and negative occurrence of the \emph{same} source variable needed to make an \emph{edited} source SAT, then
\[
\boxed{\mu(F)=d_{\rm SAT}(F)\le \lfloor m/2\rfloor=2n/3.}
\]
The proof maps a mixed selected variable to one legal sign swap with an unused positive occurrence, and pulls a satisfying selector backward from any edited SAT formula. Thus $\mu=0$ iff original SAT, so computing the optimum is not supplied by the polynomial Hall matching. The constructive \emph{SAT-edit} algorithm charges $O(N/w+n+m)$ reads, $O(m\sqrt{n+m})$ matching, $O(n+m)$ source edits/replay, $O(N)$ output bits and $O(n+m)$ live words. This edit intentionally changes the SAT label and does not solve unchanged SAT.
On the real D\"ocker source $U$ ($n=198,m=264,N=15870$) and Role 4 two-sign SAT neighbor $V$, the prior certified UNSAT proof of $U$ plus direct $V$ witness replay yields $\mu(U)=1$, $\mu(V)=0$. A deterministic unrestricted Hall matching on $U$ is nonoptimal, with 42 mixed original source variables and a 42-swap SAT edit. \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} complete original source/sign checks and 300 small positive brute-force controls, zero failed assertions; the earlier UNSAT proof is imported, not rerun. \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED:} an unsigned Hall-capacity match, large semantic sign-repair distance, or an expensive chosen representation establishes necessary superpolynomial SAT continuation. \textbf{OPEN:} all-decider lower-bound or general uniform polynomial algorithm, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$. Verified standalone:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-24_0317_LSPV-2_POLARITY_REPAIR63.tex}.


\section{LSPV-2 (backward algorithms and charged normalization)}

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (DUALPAIR61; independent review pending).}
For a proper unique signed exact-$(2,2)$ original-source 3CNF, canonical opposite-monotone support pairing has the globally optimal defect
$t_{\min}=m-2\sum_S\min(p_S,q_S)$ within the explicit
positive/negative-support extraction calculus. Exact $(2,2)$ occurrence
counts guarantee every variable belongs to at most two extracted supports;
signed source counts and the output certificate are constructed and checked
in $O(m\log(m+2))$ word time and $O(n+m)$ words. The defect is even
because $m=4n/3$ is divisible by four, \emph{not} because the input
necessarily has equally many monotone positive and negative clauses.
Two valid source-only SAT families have equal $n=3q$, $m=4q$, equal encoded
length $N$, and opposite parameter extremes:
$(t_{\min},\delta)=(0,4q)$ for prism paired-NAE supports and
$(4q,0)$ for two pivot implication cycles. Both have linear-time
direct witnesses. This \textbf{FALSIFIES/RETIRES} any unconditional
time-cost inference from either optional defect parameter alone.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 1,375 checks, zero failures; 60 random strict
signed-source controls, 863 small two-pin extension checks and two
non-promise both-label controls. A universal two-pin extension theorem
is only \textbf{CANDIDATE/OPEN}; 198/264 genuine strict signed-regular
UNSAT source and SAT mutant remain historical DOCKER-QUOTIENT59 anchors
and were not rerun. \textbf{OPEN:} universal all-decider acquisition,
representation or continuation cost, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.
Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-24_0051_LSPV-2_DUALPAIR61.tex};
executable:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-24_0051_DUALPAIR61_minirepro.py}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (BLOSSOM-FACTOR60; independent review pending):}
A source-exact \emph{nonbipartite} interval-factor continuation now
completes the implementational gap left by WITNESS-DEFECT58.
For every free original source edge $e=uv$, a weighted-matching gadget
has two required endpoint stubs and $U_v$ slots at each support vertex,
of which $L_v$ are required.  An auxiliary edge has weight equal to
the number of its required endpoints.  A feasible source-coherent factor
exists iff an integer-weight blossom maximum matching attains the
total weight $|R|$, with both implications proved by matching stub
pairs versus matching both stubs into endpoint slots.
Canonical greedy pairing of positive/negative source triple supports,
using each source variable in at most two selected supports, leaves
$t$ true source defects and gives an exact algorithm for every
proper CNF of width at most three in
\[
O\bigl(N/w+m\log(m+2)+3^t(n+m)^3\bigr)
\]
word operations.  This is a charged \emph{upper} bound with original
source reconstruction, not a lower bound.  \textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:}
2,238 assertion groups, zero failures; same-size SAT/UNSAT K4 and
prism controls; a genuinely global matching-shortfall $2<3$
despite nonempty local degree intervals; 140 general proper
CNF controls; nonbipartite supports through 147 original variables.
The six-defect augmentations are not globally strict signed-$(2,2)$.
\textbf{OPEN:} all-input polynomial small-defect normalization,
D\"ocker's 198-variable strict-monotone UNSAT source audit,
independent peer review, a universal mandatory-cost bridge,
$P=NP$, and $P\ne NP$.  The paired-support defect $t$ is distinct
from BOUNDARY-EXCEPTIONS59's pivot-defect $\delta$.
Verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-23_2314_LSPV-2_BLOSSOM_FACTOR60.tex}.



\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (BOUNDARY-EXCEPTIONS59, independent review pending):}
For proper signed exact-$(2^+,2^-)$ 3CNF, PAIRPATH73 contracts $p$
forced complementary clause pairs and projects all internal binary paths
and cycles. Set $\delta=m-2p$, and let $U$ be the source-variable
boundary of the $\delta$ unmatched genuine ternaries. The projected
$P\le3\delta/4$ binary clauses have disjoint variable supports, each
endpoint appearing twice with opposite polarity in the unmatched ternaries.
Exact signed occurrence conservation sharpens the PAIRPATH bound to
\[
|U|=3\delta/2-a\le3\delta/2,
\]
where $a$ counts boundary variables occurring four times in the ternaries.
Every satisfiable projected kernel has a satisfying assignment with
exactly one TRUE \emph{signed literal} per projected binary: changing
a second TRUE endpoint to FALSE only turns its negative-polarity
ternary appearances TRUE. This preserves the one-bit SAT label,
not all original source assignments.
Choosing one independent fair bit for each such binary and each other
boundary variable makes every ternary bad event either impossible
or probability $1/8$. A bad event conflicts with at most six others,
because each of its three failure requirements has at most two
opposite source occurrences. Therefore
\[
\Pr[\mathrm{no\ ternary\ fails}]
\ge\begin{cases}1-\delta/8,&\delta<8,\\1/64,&\delta=8.\end{cases}
\]
In particular every source-valid $\delta\le8$ instance is SAT,
and any UNSAT source has even defect $\delta\ge10$. This strengthens
PAIRPATH73's former $\delta+2P<8$ sufficient-SAT criterion.
A source-checkable optimized normalizer uses
$O(m\log(m+2))$ word work and $O(n+m)$ reusable words;
optional witness recovery enumerates at most
$2^{|U|-P}\le4096$ boundary samples at $\delta\le8$,
uses SCC to lift the source assignment, and replays all original clauses.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 360 random balanced sources,
144 valid abstract kernels, 840 exact boundary projection checks,
87 resolution attempts (82 tautology discharges), 3,712 evaluator gate
checks, and 5,928 source-replay literal reads; zero final assertion failures.
The five scalable Role-4 SAT/UNSAT controls do not themselves satisfy
the signed exact-$(2,2)$ promise. The D\"ocker 198-variable/264-clause
strict-monotone UNSAT source is a published existence theorem,
not computationally reverified here; the journal calls it Proposition 2,
correcting some stale project citations of Proposition 1.
\textbf{OPEN:} a source-aware mandatory-cost bridge against every exact
uniform deterministic SAT decider, and both $P=NP$ and $P\ne NP$.
Readback-verified report:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-23_2212_LSPV-2_BOUNDARY_EXCEPTIONS59.tex}.
Reproducible code and raw summary share its run prefix.

$3^t\operatorname{poly}(N)$ exact source-coherent decision and witness

Pair discovery, rejected witness choices, factor work, original-source replay

COMPUTATIONAL: 162 checked assertions, zero failures; 14 same-size

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted (REVERSE-SELECTOR57, independent peer review pending):}
For a proper source-coherent 3-CNF, backward evaluation of the AND-of-OR
circuit yields one \emph{selected witness occurrence} per clause,
not an exactly-one constraint on original literal truth.  A consistent
selection exists iff the source CNF is SAT. For exact signed
$(2^+,2^-)$ occurrence 3-CNF, $4n=3m$; the complement-conflict graph
is the disjoint union of $n$ copies of $K_{2,2}$, with $4n$ vertices
and $4n$ edges. Adding $m$ clause triangles gives exactly $8n$
edges and degree four, and an independent set of size $m$ iff SAT.
The fan-in-two \emph{evaluation} circuit has $3m-1$ gates and
$6m-2$ directed gate-input wires; its functional Tseitin trace has
$9m-2$ clauses, but none of these counts is a SAT-solving lower bound.

A further constructive restricted result: if positive and negative
triple supports are paired exactly, their cubic source-sharing graph
can be satisfied by taking an arbitrary \emph{maximal} matching $M$,
then matching each unmatched vertex to a distinct matched neighbor.
Hall holds since $3|Z|\le2|N(Z)|$ for unmatched sets.
Color source edges in the two matchings TRUE and all other source
edges FALSE. Every paired triple receives one or two TRUE variables.
This yields $O(n^2)$ charged word time and $O(n+m)$ space, with original
source assignment reconstruction and clause replay.
For $t$ unpaired signed clauses, branch on at most $3t$ actual
source variables and solve the remaining NAE degree intervals via
integral circulation when the remaining source-support graph is
bipartite. This gives a checked
$2^{3t}\operatorname{poly}(N)$ upper bound with full source coherence;
the executable does not yet contain the nonbipartite general-factor
algorithm. Older paired satisfiability and independent-transversal
equivalence were already known; the constructive matching/defect
pipeline is the run's new restricted algorithmic contribution.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} 353 assertion groups, zero failures; source,
circuit and functional Tseitin exhaustion through $q=5$,
scalable paired tests through $q=40$, nonmaximal greedy matching and
mismatched-support/flow controls. The UNSAT proper-3CNF hostile
controls are \emph{not} themselves exact signed $(2^+,2^-)$; the
published exact-regular UNSAT instance is not yet independently audited.
\textbf{OPEN:} a general misaligned exact-regular source solver or
any compulsory rich-representation/equally-expensive-shortcut theorem
for every deterministic SAT decider. Neither $P=NP$ nor $P\ne NP$
is established. Verified source:
\path{research/agents/lspv-2/2026-10-23_1912_LSPV-2_REVERSE_SELECTOR57.tex}.

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, AFFINE-INTERFACE54:}
For any width-three CNF, sound signed-clause recognition extracts $g$

$2^{h+t}\operatorname{poly}(N)$-time decision procedure, with

A matched connected family has $n=6+2g$, $k=12+4g$ ternaries, $h=1$,

Conversely, XOR3-parity-one plus three binary at-most-one clauses

\par\noindent\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted, FACTOR-JOIN56:}

The matched connected source formulas $F_{p,q}^{\pm}$ have

so $k=6,m=8,q=2$ with two \emph{partial} complementary pairs and balanced

\]
It rejects boundary $001$ and $110$, has six feasible words, full unary/pairwise

inside $\mathcal C_\star$ have $k=6r,m=8r,q=2r$, $24^r$ full models and $6^r$
feasible boundary words against $8^r$ pairwise-relaxed words. A source-readable

enumeration of these states. The restricted independent-hidden-witness gluing
rule, pairwise-hull recovery rule and implicit-candidate-count work potential
are \textbf{FALSIFIED/RETIRED}. These are not universal SAT decision lower bounds.

\subsection*{LSPV-3: Semantic joins, shared-witness coherence and adaptive representations}

\paragraph{M22-MAXDEFECT-FACTOR101 (25 October 2026): maximally unpaired connected source with huge frontier and a constant exact boundary relation.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT in the explicitly declared source construction and unchanged-original-clause frontier model; independent mathematical review pending.}
Partition the edges of a connected simple four-regular bipartite graph
$G$ on $2r$ vertices into four perfect matchings. Group the two positive-factor
edges and two negative-factor edges into pairs labelled by $r$ fresh original
$A$ variables. For every factor edge $uv$, emit the proper monotone clause
$(a_i\vee u\vee v)$ or $(\neg a_i\vee\neg u\vee\neg v)$ according to its factor.
The resulting source $F_G\in\mathcal C_\star$ has
\[
 k=3r,\qquad m=4r,\qquad
 |P\cap Q|=0,\qquad q=2r=2k/3.
\]
Thus its source is connected, signed-occurrence-balanced and \emph{maximally
unpaired}. Assigning $B_L=1$, $B_R=0$ satisfies all clauses for every
assignment to the $r$ boundary $A$ names. Hence its exact projected
feasibility predicate is $Q(A)\equiv1$ even though it admits $2^r$
boundary words. A fully charged source syntax recognizer and direct
witness check therefore decide this recognized family in polynomial time.
For four-regular bipartite edge expanders, \emph{every} unchanged original
signed-clause order exposes at least $h_0(r-1)/4=\Omega(N/\log N)$ live
original $B$ source names, without forcing comparable writable bits or
post-acquisition machine work. This strengthens the earlier M22-MATCH-EXPANDER100
$q=2$ example by simultaneously taking $q$ to its maximum value.

\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL:} full independent local standard-library checks for
$r=4,5,6,8,12,16,24$, 112 shuffled clause orderings and 1940
assertion-accounted groups with zero discrepancies; $r=4,5$ full model
counts are 346 and 1304. Finite test graphs were not certified to be an
expander family and physical FLMV Turing bit operations, flips, peak live
writable bits and cumulative bit-time were not measured.
Two conventional proper-ternary forced-literal pin levels have
$k_d=3r+2d$, $m_d=4r+4d$ and \emph{growing} ordinary child encoding length;
both descendants violate the $\mathcal C_\star$ grammar. A declared
four-leaf independent exhaustive residual list fixing two source bits has
$\Psi=2^k$, $R_{\rm search}=0$; this is not a compulsory decision budget.

\textbf{OPEN:} ARC-EDGE-90 all-input, jointly achievable, same-machine charged
finite route into strict FLMV box
$t<241/200,\ s<11642479/23232400$; DIRECT-POST independently initialized
worst-case complete-state potential robust to all adaptive methods; general
target-preserving recursive adapter; P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.
The fully paired recognizable subclass is excluded from $\mathcal C_\star$
but partial pairs remain allowed; the six-variable source $Y$ is in-target
SAT, the source U of D\"ocker is in-target UNSAT by published certificate
(not independently solved here). Old universal cosine product endpoint
is retired. Exact original report, compact peer reproducer, finite raw

\paragraph{PIN-BIT-FLOOR98 (25 October 2026): ordinary independent pin-child serialization floor, restricted.}
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT only for the fixed source-name-preserving eager
four-clause-per-pin compiler; independent mathematical review pending.}
Combine EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97's all-clause-order source-frontier
bound with SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92's actual proper-3CNF pin syntax.
For a cubic $h_0$-edge expander Tseitin instance with $v$ vertices
and source bit length
$N=(16v+2)\lceil\log_2(64(11v/2+1))\rceil$,
every contiguous sublinear-clause block partition, regardless of
clause permutation, has a cut with
$w\ge h_0v/2-o(v)$ live original edge names.  An eager
ordinary pinned child incident to that cut contains at least $4w$
proper clauses and $2w$ fresh auxiliary variables; hence
\[
 N_{\rm child}\ge(16w+2)
  \left\lceil\log_2\bigl(64(6w+1)\bigr)\right\rceil
 =\Omega(N).
\]
This refutes a universal \emph{power-short ordinary-child guarantee for
that specific unchanged-clause, eager-pin representation}, even after
adaptive source-clause ordering.  It does not refute a semantic
normalizer or an existential all-input achievable-pair closure.  An
independently validated affine parity recognizer solves the same
matched SAT/UNSAT Tseitin sources directly, so neither source width
nor materialization of pinned children is mandatory general SAT work.
Ordinary child bit encoding $(4m_c+2)h_c$ is distinguished from tagged
scoped-stream $(2+4n_c+4m_c)h_c$ and from indexed virtual handles.
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL}: seven finite cubic-prism sizes, 224 shuffled
source-clause orders, 923 assertion groups with zero failures;
minimum sampled eager child/source bit ratio $>1.8$.  These finite
prisms are not an asymptotic expander family and the Python counters
are not FLMV physical bit-work/space traces.
\textbf{OPEN}: source-aware \emph{all-input} favorable joint
SAT resource successor or cofinal charged route into the genuine FLMV box.

\noindent\textbf{Latest Role 3: TYPED-DEGREE-BRIDGE98, PROVED/EXACT restricted (independent review pending).}
For a source containing signed exact-one ternary modules $E(\ell_1,\ell_2,\ell_3)$ and named binary equality records, define the \emph{typed} degree of a variable as the count of its original module/equality appearances, not the number of post-compilation ordinary-CNF literals. With maximum typed degree two, equality components are paths, cycles or isolated vertices. Contracting each component gives at most two exact-one occurrences per variable; repeated-coordinate constraints reduce by truth table, and the remaining signed degree-two exact-cardinality problem is solved in polynomial time by the source-matched INTERVAL-FACTOR134 graph-factor method.

For maximum typed degree three, an equality-only intermodule join of \emph{three-state} local exact-one modules remains NP-complete even with a connected constraint graph, every variable in exactly one exact-one module, and preserved source names. The new neutral two-module bridge between original disconnected components consists of $E(u',z_1,a)$, $E(v',z_2,b)$ and $u'=u,z_1=z_2,v'=v$. The assignment $z_1=z_2=0$, $a=1-u$, $b=1-v$ extends every original assignment, so bridging does not change SAT status. For an original positive 1-in-3-SAT source with $m$ triples, $n_+$ occurring variables and $c$ incidence components, the resulting connected typed source has
\[
 M=m+2(c-1),\quad L=3m-n_++3(c-1)
\]
exact-one modules and named equality links. Compiling to ordinary proper three-CNF gives
\[
 n'=4M+2L,\qquad m'=6M+4L,\qquad
 N'=(4m'+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(n'+m'+1))\right\rceil .
\]
A connected matched SAT/UNSAT control has $M=7,L=11,n'=50,m'=86,N'=4844$ with identical unsigned support. This refines existing DEGREE-MATCH-LADDER132 and EXACTONE-JOIN-CYCLE95 rather than claiming a new original degree-three NP-completeness theorem.

\noindent\textbf{Computational and adaptive ledger.}
A full reproducible standard-library audit tested 160 typed joins (139 SAT, 21 UNSAT, including 68 originally disconnected sources) and 260 typed degree-two controls (234 SAT, 26 UNSAT). It recorded 3,896 checks, zero discrepancies, and two identical raw outputs with SHA-256 \texttt{f7e944c01d3f11618ef2a9843a6adc7b6928dea8a4d8bf5c368a6b4eb6faca4d}. Finite computation is not an asymptotic machine theorem. The uniform source-language upper bound $2^{k(I)}\operatorname{poly}(|I|)$ with $k(I)=|\{v:d_I(v)\ge3\}|$ is representation dependent. A complete BIT-RESOURCE1 ledger must charge source bits, parameter discovery, strategy selection/switching, failed alternatives, matching/factor search, name-copy equality, ordinary child serialization, caches, witness replay when used, reads and writes including unchanged values, actual state flips, peak live bits, cumulative bit-time and code size.

\noindent\textbf{Receiving ARC/FLMV obligation: OPEN.}
These restricted algorithms and NP-complete connected joins establish no all-input jointly achieved SAT time-space improvement. Neither local support three nor maximum typed degree three forces computation by every algorithm. Ordinary compilation has source-scale children, not the requisite $N^{\lambda+o(1)}$ ordinary SAT children for $\lambda<1$. The highest-value next test is SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 and OVERLAP-CUT128 on the bridged pair and STRICT-SUBJOIN-STATUS131, over two recursion levels, with charged shared-variable compatibility and fully encoded child bits. The original HEREDITARY-SCOPED10 executable remains unverified. P versus NP remains \OPEN{}.

\noindent\textbf{Earlier Role 3: EXPANDER-STREAM-ORDER97, PROVED/EXACT restricted source-frontier obstruction (independent review pending).}
For a connected cubic graph $G$ with $v$ even vertices and edge-bisection width
$B(G)=\min_{|U|=v/2}|\delta_G(U)|$, encode each degree-three vertex XOR
equation as four proper ternaries on its three incident edge variables.
Then \emph{every} permutation of the original unchanged $4v$ clauses,
with the same named source edge variables, has a cut with at least
$B(G)$ simultaneously live original variables. Stop after the first
$v/2$ distinct vertex gadgets have appeared: each crossing edge
variable occurs in a processed clause and in an unprocessed clause.
On cubic expanders, this is
\[
w\ge h_0v/2=\Omega(N/\log N),\qquad
N=(16v+2)\left\lceil\log_2(64(3v/2+4v+1))\right\rceil.
\]
The clauses are balanced, with exactly four positive and four negative
occurrences for every edge variable, and no pure-literal escape.
Yet every charge vector is decided by the source-validated affine
component-parity condition
$\operatorname{SAT}(T(G,b))\iff\bigoplus_{u\in V}b_u=0$;
SAT members have $2^{|E|-v+1}$ witnesses.
All-zero and one-twist charges give matched SAT/UNSAT sources with
identical unsigned supports, source size and live-width lower bound.
This polynomial-time semantic escape was already known in
PARITY-SHORTCUT78 and CUBIC-AFFINE-FIREWALL98; the new increment is
the exact quantifier over \emph{every original clause order}.
A block-aligned frontier refinement also holds: for any partition
of the unchanged clause order into consecutive blocks of at most
$B$ clauses, some block boundary has at least
$\max\{0,B(G)-3B\}$ live source variables. Crossing one
proper ternary changes liveness of at most three variables.
For square-root clause windows $B=\lceil\sqrt{4v}\rceil$ on
constant-expansion cubic graphs, this remains $\Omega(v)$.
This directly limits clause-reordering-only versions of the
SCOPED-BLOCK-REBUILDER92 schedule, not semantic affine
normalization or arbitrary SAT algorithms.
The theorem refutes a clause-reordering-only low-width normalization,
not arbitrary source-aware rewriting or unrestricted decision time.
\nopagebreak

\noindent\textbf{Prior Role 3 result: SCOPE-ALIAS-COMPLETENESS96, exact restricted language boundary and source-name repair.}
For any ordinary proper-3CNF with $m$ clauses, emitting one
OPEN--OPEN--OPEN--CLAUSE--CLOSE--CLOSE--CLOSE block
per clause retains the exact original global signed formula
under erasure of event tags. Each block has locally live width three
and the complete reopening stream has
\[
 N_{\rm local}=(2+28m)h\le 7(4m+2)h,
 \quad h=\lceil\log_2(64(n+m+1))\rceil.
\]
SAT on this locally well-formed but \emph{globally reopening} grammar
is therefore NP-complete by identity reduction from proper-3CNF.
A frontier DP that forgets every name on CLOSE wrongly accepts every
such stream because every detached proper ternary clause is SAT.
Globally fresh occurrence names restore syntactic validity at
width three but destroy source semantics. To repair exactly,
identify copies of the same original source name with
$r=3m-\nu$ equality links, where $\nu$ names actually occur.
An ordinary proper-3CNF realization with two fresh auxiliary
variables and four ternaries per equality has
\[
 n'=3m+2r,\qquad m'=m+4r.
\]
The alias join is source-name preserving but does not furnish
genuinely power-smaller ordinary SAT children.
Every one of the 24 individual occurrences in the complete
eight-clause three-variable UNSAT core is semantically essential:
freeing any one occurrence yields SAT. This does not imply
that one arbitrary equality-tree edge always suffices.

Role 4's global-freshness validator lower bound and Role 3/5's
three-state equality-join NP-completeness remain prior restricted
results, not new attributions. If an \emph{all-input} producer
could turn each reopening stream into a \emph{globally valid}
width-three stream of $N^{1+o(1)}$ output bits with
time $N^{1.1+o(1)}$ and space $N^{.5+o(1)}$ in the same
matched FLMV model, online frontier DP would contradict the
published strict forbidden box at $c=241/200$.
This excludes the stated complete producer contract, not all
source-aware SAT algorithms, which may decide directly,
reread, cache, propagate or change representations.
\textbf{PROVED/EXACT restricted} for the typed semantic reductions,
\textbf{COMPUTATIONAL} for 585 sources, 523 SAT and 62 UNSAT,
7,196 assertion checks and 24 occurrence release tests, zero errors.
The full BIT-RESOURCE1 vector was not measured as physical
FLMV machine traces. The universal all-input ARC successor and
cofinal charged finite route both remain \OPEN{}.
